#include "fan_driver.h"
#include "esphome/core/log.h"
#include <algorithm>

static const char *const TAG = "garuda.fan";

namespace garuda {

FanDriver::FanDriver() {}

void FanDriver::init(const FanConfig &config) {
    config_ = config;

    relay_settle_timer_.set_interval_millis(config_.relay_settle_ms);
    boost_timer_.set_interval_millis(config_.boost_duration_ms);
    spindown_timeout_timer_.set_interval_millis(config_.spindown_timeout_ms);
    shutdown_dwell_timer_.set_interval_millis(config_.shutdown_dwell_ms);
    restart_lockout_timer_.set_interval_millis(config_.restart_lockout_ms);

    current_speed_pct_ = 0.0f;
    target_speed_pct_ = 0.0f;
    current_rpm_ = 0.0f;
    current_amps_ = 0.0f;
    state_ = FanRunState::STOPPED;
    is_relay_on_ = false;
    disagreement_active_ = false;
    stall_eval_window_count_ = 0;
    disagreement_eval_window_count_ = 0;
    last_slew_time_ = monotonic_micros();

    write_physical_pwm(0.0f);
    deenergize_relay();

    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Fan Driver initialized (Relay Sequencing, Slew Profiler & Triple-Window Feedback Filter Active)");
}

void FanDriver::update_config(const FanConfig &config) {
    config_ = config;
    relay_settle_timer_.set_interval_millis(config_.relay_settle_ms);
    boost_timer_.set_interval_millis(config_.boost_duration_ms);
    spindown_timeout_timer_.set_interval_millis(config_.spindown_timeout_ms);
    shutdown_dwell_timer_.set_interval_millis(config_.shutdown_dwell_ms);
    restart_lockout_timer_.set_interval_millis(config_.restart_lockout_ms);
}

void FanDriver::energize_relay() {
    if (!is_relay_on_) {
        if (relay_output_ != nullptr) {
            relay_output_->turn_on();
        }
        is_relay_on_ = true;
        ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Fan Relay ENERGIZED (12V Power ON)");
    }
}

void FanDriver::deenergize_relay() {
    if (is_relay_on_ || relay_output_ != nullptr) {
        if (relay_output_ != nullptr) {
            relay_output_->turn_off();
        }
        is_relay_on_ = false;
        restart_lockout_timer_.reset();
        ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Fan Relay DE-ENERGIZED (Power Isolated). 5s Restart lockout active (FAN-04, FAN-018).");
    }
}

void FanDriver::ingest_rpm(float rpm) {
    current_rpm_ = (rpm >= 0.0f && !std::isnan(rpm)) ? rpm : 0.0f;

    // Triple-window evaluation executed synchronously upon discrete pulse counter ingestion (FAN-03, FAN-025)
    if (config_.tach_enabled && state_ == FanRunState::RUNNING && current_speed_pct_ >= 30.0f) {
        if (current_rpm_ < config_.stall_rpm_threshold) {
            stall_eval_window_count_++;
            ESP_LOGW(TAG, "Stall warning: RPM %.0f < %.0f (Window %u of %u)",
                     current_rpm_, config_.stall_rpm_threshold, stall_eval_window_count_, config_.filter_window_count);
            if (stall_eval_window_count_ >= config_.filter_window_count) {
                state_ = FanRunState::STALLED;
                target_speed_pct_ = 0.0f;
                current_speed_pct_ = 0.0f;
                write_physical_pwm(0.0f);
                deenergize_relay();
                ESP_LOGE(TAG, "CRITICAL: Fan motor stall confirmed across %u consecutive windows. Relay cut (FAN-03, GLOB-26).",
                         config_.filter_window_count);
            }
        } else {
            stall_eval_window_count_ = 0;
        }

        // Diagnostic Disagreement evaluation: Normal current detected (>0.05A) but tachometer is near zero (<50 RPM)
        if (current_amps_ >= 0.05f && current_rpm_ < 50.0f) {
            disagreement_eval_window_count_++;
            if (disagreement_eval_window_count_ >= config_.filter_window_count) {
                if (!disagreement_active_) {
                    disagreement_active_ = true;
                    ESP_LOGW(TAG, "DIAGNOSTIC: Feedback Disagreement confirmed! Current %.2fA with zero RPM across %u windows (FAN-026).",
                             current_amps_, config_.filter_window_count);
                }
            }
        } else {
            disagreement_eval_window_count_ = 0;
            disagreement_active_ = false;
        }
    } else {
        stall_eval_window_count_ = 0;
        disagreement_eval_window_count_ = 0;
    }
}

void FanDriver::ingest_current(float amps) {
    current_amps_ = (amps >= 0.0f && !std::isnan(amps)) ? amps : 0.0f;
}

void FanDriver::latch_stall() {
    state_ = FanRunState::STALLED;
    target_speed_pct_ = 0.0f;
    current_speed_pct_ = 0.0f;
    disagreement_active_ = false;
    write_physical_pwm(0.0f);
    deenergize_relay();
    ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Fan initialized into STALLED state from persisted NVS latch (FHT-02).");
}

void FanDriver::clear_stall() {
    if (state_ == FanRunState::STALLED) {
        state_ = FanRunState::STOPPED;
        target_speed_pct_ = 0.0f;
        current_speed_pct_ = 0.0f;
        disagreement_active_ = false;
        stall_eval_window_count_ = 0;
        disagreement_eval_window_count_ = 0;
        write_physical_pwm(0.0f);
        deenergize_relay();
        ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Fan stall latch cleared. Motor unblocked.");
    }
}

void FanDriver::emergency_stop() {
    target_speed_pct_ = 0.0f;
    current_speed_pct_ = 0.0f;
    disagreement_active_ = false;
    state_ = FanRunState::STOPPED;
    write_physical_pwm(0.0f);
    deenergize_relay();
    ESP_LOGW(TAG, "EMERGENCY STOP: Relay opened and PWM cut immediately (OM-04).");
}

void FanDriver::set_target(percent_t target_pct) {
    if (state_ == FanRunState::STALLED) {
        ESP_LOGW(TAG, "Command rejected: Fan is STALLED. Clear latch first.");
        return;
    }

    target_pct = std::clamp(target_pct, 0.0f, 100.0f);
    if (std::abs(target_speed_pct_ - target_pct) < 0.1f) return;

    // 1. Command is START from full stop
    if (state_ == FanRunState::STOPPED && target_pct > 0.0f) {
        if (!restart_lockout_timer_.has_expired()) {
            ESP_LOGW(TAG, "Fan start held: Restart lockout active (waiting %lu ms for contact protection)",
                     static_cast<unsigned long>(restart_lockout_timer_.remaining_millis()));
            return;
        }

        target_speed_pct_ = target_pct;
        energize_relay();
        state_ = FanRunState::RELAY_SETTLING;
        relay_settle_timer_.reset();
        write_physical_pwm(0.0f);
        ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Fan startup initiated: Relay closed, pausing 500ms for inrush stabilization (FAN-02)...");
        return;
    }

    // 2. Shutdown Interruption (FAN-019)
    if ((state_ == FanRunState::STOPPING_SLEW || 
         state_ == FanRunState::STOPPING_SPINDOWN || 
         state_ == FanRunState::SHUTDOWN_DWELL) && target_pct > 0.0f) {
        ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Shutdown interrupted: Reusing energized relay path (FAN-019)");
        target_speed_pct_ = target_pct;
        last_slew_time_ = monotonic_micros();
        state_ = FanRunState::RAMPING;
        return;
    }

    // 3. Command is STOP
    if (target_pct <= 0.0f) {
        target_speed_pct_ = 0.0f;
        last_slew_time_ = monotonic_micros();
        state_ = FanRunState::STOPPING_SLEW;
        return;
    }

    // 4. In-flight speed change
    target_speed_pct_ = target_pct;
    last_slew_time_ = monotonic_micros();
    if (state_ != FanRunState::RELAY_SETTLING && state_ != FanRunState::STARTING_BOOST) {
        state_ = FanRunState::RAMPING;
    }
}

void FanDriver::write_physical_pwm(percent_t logical_pct) {
    if (pwm_output_ == nullptr) return;

    if (logical_pct <= 0.0f) {
        pwm_output_->set_level(0.0f);
        return;
    }

    float factor = std::clamp(logical_pct / 100.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f);
    float physical_duty_pct = config_.pwm_min + factor * (config_.pwm_max - config_.pwm_min);
    pwm_output_->set_level(std::clamp(physical_duty_pct / 100.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f));
}

void FanDriver::process() {
    monotonic_time_t now = monotonic_micros();

    // Stage 1: Relay Inrush Settling (500ms delay before applying PWM, FAN-02, FAN-011)
    if (state_ == FanRunState::RELAY_SETTLING) {
        if (relay_settle_timer_.has_expired()) {
            if (config_.boost_enabled && target_speed_pct_ < 20.0f) {
                state_ = FanRunState::STARTING_BOOST;
                boost_timer_.reset();
                write_physical_pwm(config_.boost_pwm);
                ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Inrush settled. Applying 2.0s startup boost (%.1f%%) to break friction (FAN-012)...",
                         config_.boost_pwm);
            } else {
                state_ = FanRunState::RAMPING;
                last_slew_time_ = now;
                ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Inrush settled. Ramping PWM toward target %.1f%%...", target_speed_pct_);
            }
        }
        return;
    }

    // Stage 2: Startup Boost (2.0s @ 20% PWM, FAN-012)
    if (state_ == FanRunState::STARTING_BOOST) {
        if (boost_timer_.has_expired()) {
            state_ = FanRunState::RAMPING;
            current_speed_pct_ = config_.boost_pwm;
            last_slew_time_ = now;
            stall_eval_window_count_ = 0;
            disagreement_eval_window_count_ = 0;
            ESP_LOGD(TAG, "Startup boost completed. Transitioning to target ramp.");
        }
        return;
    }

    // Stage 3: Slew Rate Profiler (FAN-08)
    if (std::abs(current_speed_pct_ - target_speed_pct_) > 0.1f) {
        float elapsed_sec = static_cast<float>(now - last_slew_time_) / 1000000.0f;
        last_slew_time_ = now;

        float max_step = config_.slew_rate_pct_per_sec * elapsed_sec;
        if (max_step > 0.05f) {
            if (current_speed_pct_ < target_speed_pct_) {
                current_speed_pct_ = std::min(current_speed_pct_ + max_step, target_speed_pct_);
            } else {
                current_speed_pct_ = std::max(current_speed_pct_ - max_step, target_speed_pct_);
            }
            write_physical_pwm(current_speed_pct_);
        }
    } else {
        current_speed_pct_ = target_speed_pct_;
        if (current_speed_pct_ > 0.0f) {
            state_ = FanRunState::RUNNING;
        } else if (state_ == FanRunState::STOPPING_SLEW) {
            write_physical_pwm(0.0f);
            if (config_.tach_enabled) {
                state_ = FanRunState::STOPPING_SPINDOWN;
                spindown_timeout_timer_.reset();
                ESP_LOGI(TAG, "PWM reached 0%%. Awaiting physical motor spin-down confirmation (FAN-04, FAN-016)...");
            } else {
                state_ = FanRunState::SHUTDOWN_DWELL;
                shutdown_dwell_timer_.reset();
                ESP_LOGI(TAG, "PWM reached 0%% (Tachless). Holding relay ON for 10.0s thermal dissipation (FAN-017)...");
            }
        }
    }

    // Stage 4: Controlled Spin-Down Confirmation (FAN-04, FAN-016)
    if (state_ == FanRunState::STOPPING_SPINDOWN) {
        bool motor_stopped = (current_rpm_ < 50.0f);
        bool timeout_expired = spindown_timeout_timer_.has_expired();

        if (motor_stopped || timeout_expired) {
            if (timeout_expired) {
                ESP_LOGW(TAG, "Spin-down confirmation timeout (15s) elapsed. Proceeding to dissipation dwell.");
            } else {
                ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Motor halt confirmed (RPM: %.0f < 50). Proceeding to dissipation dwell.", current_rpm_);
            }
            state_ = FanRunState::SHUTDOWN_DWELL;
            shutdown_dwell_timer_.reset();
        }
        return;
    }

    // Stage 5: Shutdown Dissipation Dwell Completion (FAN-04, FAN-017)
    if (state_ == FanRunState::SHUTDOWN_DWELL) {
        if (shutdown_dwell_timer_.has_expired()) {
            deenergize_relay();
            state_ = FanRunState::STOPPED;
        }
        return;
    }
}

} // namespace garuda

#pragma once

#include "garuda_types.h"
#include "monotonic_clock.h"
#include "esphome/components/output/float_output.h"
#include "esphome/components/output/binary_output.h"

namespace garuda {

struct FanConfig {
    percent_t pwm_min{20.0f};           // Physical PWM floor when running (FAN-01)
    percent_t pwm_max{100.0f};          // Physical PWM ceiling
    float slew_rate_pct_per_sec{10.0f}; // Slew rate profile (FAN-08)
    bool boost_enabled{true};           // Low-speed boost enabled (FAN-02)
    percent_t boost_pwm{20.0f};         // Boost duty level
    uint32_t relay_settle_ms{500};      // 500ms inrush stabilization delay (FAN-02, FAN-011)
    uint32_t boost_duration_ms{2000};   // 2.0s boost duration (FAN-012)
    uint32_t shutdown_dwell_ms{10000};  // 10.0s dissipation delay before power cut (FAN-04, FAN-017)
    uint32_t restart_lockout_ms{5000};  // Lockout timer from actual relay de-energization (FAN-04, FAN-018)
    uint32_t spindown_timeout_ms{15000};// Maximum wait for rotor stop confirmation (FAN-04)
    bool tach_enabled{true};            // Tachometer monitoring (FAN-027)
    float stall_rpm_threshold{100.0f};  // Minimum RPM expected at >= 30% duty
    uint8_t filter_window_count{3};     // Triple consecutive evaluation windows (FAN-03, FAN-025)
};

class FanDriver {
public:
    FanDriver();

    void set_output(esphome::output::FloatOutput *pwm_out) { pwm_output_ = pwm_out; }
    void set_relay(esphome::output::BinaryOutput *relay_out) { relay_output_ = relay_out; }

    void init(const FanConfig &config);
    void update_config(const FanConfig &config);

    void set_target(percent_t target_pct);

    // Dual feedback ingestion (FAN-022)
    void ingest_rpm(float rpm);
    void ingest_current(float amps);

    void process();
    void emergency_stop();
    void clear_stall();
    void latch_stall(); // Restores persisted NVS latch at boot (FHT-02)

    // State getters
    percent_t get_current_speed() const { return current_speed_pct_; }
    percent_t get_target_speed() const { return target_speed_pct_; }
    float get_rpm() const { return current_rpm_; }
    float get_current_amps() const { return current_amps_; }
    FanRunState get_run_state() const { return state_; }
    bool is_stalled() const { return state_ == FanRunState::STALLED; }
    bool is_running() const { 
        return current_speed_pct_ > 0.0f || 
               state_ == FanRunState::RELAY_SETTLING || 
               state_ == FanRunState::STARTING_BOOST; 
    }
    bool is_relay_energized() const { return is_relay_on_; }
    bool is_disagreement_active() const { return disagreement_active_; }

private:
    void write_physical_pwm(percent_t logical_pct);
    void energize_relay();
    void deenergize_relay();

    esphome::output::FloatOutput *pwm_output_{nullptr};
    esphome::output::BinaryOutput *relay_output_{nullptr};
    FanConfig config_;

    percent_t current_speed_pct_{0.0f};
    percent_t target_speed_pct_{0.0f};
    float current_rpm_{0.0f};
    float current_amps_{0.0f};
    FanRunState state_{FanRunState::STOPPED};
    bool is_relay_on_{false};
    bool disagreement_active_{false};

    // Triple-window evaluation filters (FAN-03, FAN-025)
    uint8_t stall_eval_window_count_{0};
    uint8_t disagreement_eval_window_count_{0};

    monotonic_time_t last_slew_time_{0};
    ElapsedTimer relay_settle_timer_{500000ULL};
    ElapsedTimer boost_timer_{2000000ULL};
    ElapsedTimer spindown_timeout_timer_{15000000ULL};
    ElapsedTimer shutdown_dwell_timer_{10000000ULL};
    ElapsedTimer restart_lockout_timer_{5000000ULL};
};

} // namespace garuda

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <cmath>
#include <string>

namespace garuda {

// Canonical physical unit scalar types
using kelvin_t         = float;
using pascals_t        = float;
using liters_t         = float;
using percent_t        = float;
using monotonic_time_t = uint64_t; // Microseconds from boot

// Flap Physical Movement States (FLP-04, FLAP-032)
enum class FlapMovementState : uint8_t {
    CLOSED   = 0,
    OPENING  = 1,
    OPEN     = 2,
    CLOSING  = 3,
    SETTLING = 4
};

// Fan Operating States (FAN-01..05, FAN-016)
enum class FanRunState : uint8_t {
    STOPPED           = 0,
    RELAY_SETTLING    = 1,
    STARTING_BOOST    = 2,
    RAMPING           = 3,
    RUNNING           = 4,
    STOPPING_SLEW     = 5,
    STOPPING_SPINDOWN = 6,
    SHUTDOWN_DWELL    = 7,
    STALLED           = 8
};

// Logical Resource Identifiers (GLOB-32, CORE-020)
enum class ResourceId : uint8_t {
    RES_INTERNAL_TEMP = 0,
    RES_INTERNAL_HUM  = 1,
    RES_EXTERNAL_TEMP = 2,
    RES_EXTERNAL_HUM  = 3,
    RES_FAN_CURRENT   = 4,
    RES_BUS_VOLTAGE   = 5,
    RES_COUNT         = 6
};

// Supported Ingestion Units
enum class UnitType : uint8_t {
    KELVIN      = 0,
    CELSIUS     = 1,
    FAHRENHEIT  = 2,
    PERCENT     = 3,
    AMPERES     = 4,
    VOLTS       = 5
};

// Normalized Resource Data Quality Tags (GLOB-07, GLOB-43)
enum class QualityTag : uint8_t {
    GOOD          = 0,
    STALE         = 1,
    INVALID       = 2,
    TIMEOUT       = 3,
    DISCONNECTED  = 4,
    UNAVAILABLE   = 5,
    UNKNOWN       = 6
};

// Universal Operational Matrix Outcomes (GLOB-38, GLOB-39)
enum class OmOutcome : uint8_t {
    PERMITTED   = 0,
    DEGRADED    = 1,
    UNAVAILABLE = 2,
    PROHIBITED  = 3
};

// Universal Command Outcomes (CEP-03)
enum class CmdOutcome : uint8_t {
    REJECTED  = 0,
    BLOCKED   = 1,
    PENDING   = 2,
    APPLIED   = 3,
    FAILED    = 4,
    TIMED_OUT = 5
};

// Consolidated System Health State (GLOB-19)
enum class SystemHealthState : uint8_t {
    OK           = 0,
    HUB_OFFLINE  = 1,
    SENSOR_LOST  = 2,
    MOTOR_STALL  = 3,
    LOCKOUT      = 4
};

// Standard Machine-Readable Reason Codes (GLOB-25, FAN-026, AUT-04)
enum class ReasonCode : uint16_t {
    OK                      = 0,
    INITIALIZING            = 1,
    ESTOP_ACTIVE            = 10,
    LOCKOUT_ACTIVE          = 11,
    MOTOR_STALL             = 12,
    FLAP_INTERLOCK_BLOCKED  = 13,
    TACH_DISAGREEMENT       = 14,
    SENSOR_TIMEOUT          = 20,
    SENSOR_INVALID          = 21,
    INTERNAL_SENSOR_FAULT   = 22,
    OUTDOOR_NOT_USEFUL      = 30,
    HUB_TIMEOUT             = 40,
    COMMAND_REJECTED        = 50
};

} // namespace garuda

#include "monotonic_clock.h"
#include "esp_timer.h"

namespace garuda {

monotonic_time_t monotonic_micros() {
    return static_cast<monotonic_time_t>(esp_timer_get_time());
}

uint32_t monotonic_millis() {
    return static_cast<uint32_t>(esp_timer_get_time() / 1000ULL);
}

uint32_t monotonic_seconds() {
    return static_cast<uint32_t>(esp_timer_get_time() / 1000000ULL);
}

// --- ElapsedTimer Implementation ---

ElapsedTimer::ElapsedTimer() 
    : start_time_(monotonic_micros()), interval_micros_(0) {}

ElapsedTimer::ElapsedTimer(uint64_t interval_micros)
    : start_time_(monotonic_micros()), interval_micros_(interval_micros) {}

void ElapsedTimer::set_interval_micros(uint64_t interval_micros) {
    interval_micros_ = interval_micros;
    reset();
}

void ElapsedTimer::set_interval_millis(uint32_t interval_millis) {
    set_interval_micros(static_cast<uint64_t>(interval_millis) * 1000ULL);
}

void ElapsedTimer::set_interval_seconds(uint32_t interval_seconds) {
    set_interval_micros(static_cast<uint64_t>(interval_seconds) * 1000000ULL);
}

bool ElapsedTimer::has_expired() const {
    return elapsed_micros() >= interval_micros_;
}

bool ElapsedTimer::has_expired_and_reset() {
    if (has_expired()) {
        reset();
        return true;
    }
    return false;
}

void ElapsedTimer::reset() {
    start_time_ = monotonic_micros();
}

uint64_t ElapsedTimer::elapsed_micros() const {
    monotonic_time_t now = monotonic_micros();
    return (now >= start_time_) ? (now - start_time_) : 0;
}

uint32_t ElapsedTimer::elapsed_millis() const {
    return static_cast<uint32_t>(elapsed_micros() / 1000ULL);
}

uint64_t ElapsedTimer::remaining_micros() const {
    uint64_t el = elapsed_micros();
    return (el < interval_micros_) ? (interval_micros_ - el) : 0;
}

uint32_t ElapsedTimer::remaining_millis() const {
    return static_cast<uint32_t>(remaining_micros() / 1000ULL);
}

// --- DeadlineTimer Implementation ---

DeadlineTimer::DeadlineTimer() 
    : deadline_(0), active_(false) {}

void DeadlineTimer::set_deadline_micros(uint64_t timeout_micros) {
    deadline_ = monotonic_micros() + timeout_micros;
    active_ = true;
}

void DeadlineTimer::set_deadline_millis(uint32_t timeout_millis) {
    set_deadline_micros(static_cast<uint64_t>(timeout_millis) * 1000ULL);
}

void DeadlineTimer::set_deadline_seconds(uint32_t timeout_seconds) {
    set_deadline_micros(static_cast<uint64_t>(timeout_seconds) * 1000000ULL);
}

bool DeadlineTimer::is_expired() const {
    if (!active_) return false;
    return monotonic_micros() >= deadline_;
}

bool DeadlineTimer::is_active() const {
    return active_;
}

void DeadlineTimer::disarm() {
    active_ = false;
    deadline_ = 0;
}

uint64_t DeadlineTimer::remaining_micros() const {
    if (!active_) return 0;
    monotonic_time_t now = monotonic_micros();
    return (deadline_ > now) ? (deadline_ - now) : 0;
}

} // namespace garuda

FILE: E:\GARUDA\git\garuda-controls\sentinel\components\garuda_core\monotonic_clock.h, encoding=UTF-8
#pragma once

#include "garuda_types.h"
#include <cstdint>

namespace garuda {

// Base monotonic time getters
monotonic_time_t monotonic_micros();
uint32_t monotonic_millis();
uint32_t monotonic_seconds();

// In-memory non-blocking elapsed interval timer (SCH-01)
class ElapsedTimer {
public:

FILE: E:\GARUDA\git\garuda-controls\sentinel\components\garuda_core\operational_matrix.h, encoding=UTF-8
#pragma once

#include "garuda_types.h"
#include <cstddef>

namespace garuda {

// Single Boolean predicate rule (GLOB-37)
struct OmPredicate {
    bool condition_met;
    OmOutcome failure_outcome;
    ReasonCode failure_reason;
};

// Consolidated outcome from evaluating a matrix
struct OmResult {
    OmOutcome outcome{OmOutcome::PERMITTED};
    ReasonCode reason{ReasonCode::OK};

    bool is_permitted() const {
        return outcome == OmOutcome::PERMITTED || outcome == OmOutcome::DEGRADED;
    }
    bool is_prohibited() const {
        return outcome == OmOutcome::PROHIBITED;
    }
};

class OperationalMatrix {
public:
    // Evaluates an array of predicates using conjunctive AND semantics (GLOB-37, GLOB-39)
    static OmResult evaluate(const OmPredicate *predicates, size_t count) {
        OmResult result;
        result.outcome = OmOutcome::PERMITTED;
        result.reason = ReasonCode::OK;

        for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
            if (!predicates[i].condition_met) {
                // Escalate outcome based on universal severity:
                // PERMITTED (0) < DEGRADED (1) < UNAVAILABLE (2) < PROHIBITED (3)
                if (static_cast<uint8_t>(predicates[i].failure_outcome) > static_cast<uint8_t>(result.outcome)) {
                    result.outcome = predicates[i].failure_outcome;
                    result.reason = predicates[i].failure_reason;
                }
            }
        }
        return result;
    }
};

} // namespace garuda

FILE: E:\GARUDA\git\garuda-controls\sentinel\components\garuda_core\persistence_manager.cpp, encoding=UTF-8
#include "persistence_manager.h"
#include "nvs_flash.h"
#include "nvs.h"
#include "esphome/core/log.h"

static const char *const TAG = "garuda.persistence";

namespace garuda {

bool PersistenceManager::init() {
    esp_err_t err = nvs_flash_init();
    if (err == ESP_ERR_NVS_NO_FREE_PAGES || err == ESP_ERR_NVS_NEW_VERSION_FOUND) {
        ESP_LOGW(TAG, "NVS partition truncated/corrupt. Erasing partition...");
        nvs_flash_erase();
        err = nvs_flash_init();
    }
    if (err != ESP_OK) {
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Failed to initialize NVS flash: 0x%X", err);
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

bool PersistenceManager::load_blob(const char *namespace_name, const char *key, void *dest, size_t len) {
    nvs_handle_t handle;
    esp_err_t err = nvs_open(namespace_name, NVS_READONLY, &handle);
    if (err != ESP_OK) {
        return false;
    }

    size_t required_len = len;
    err = nvs_get_blob(handle, key, dest, &required_len);
    nvs_close(handle);

    return (err == ESP_OK && required_len == len);
}

bool PersistenceManager::save_blob(const char *namespace_name, const char *key, const void *src, size_t len) {
    nvs_handle_t handle;
    esp_err_t err = nvs_open(namespace_name, NVS_READWRITE, &handle);
    if (err != ESP_OK) {
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Failed to open NVS namespace '%s': 0x%X", namespace_name, err);
        return false;
    }

    err = nvs_set_blob(handle, key, src, len);
    if (err == ESP_OK) {
        err = nvs_commit(handle);
    }
    nvs_close(handle);

    return (err == ESP_OK);
}

uint32_t PersistenceManager::calculate_crc32(const uint8_t *data, size_t len) {
    uint32_t crc = 0xFFFFFFFF;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < len; ++i) {
        crc ^= data[i];
        for (int j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
            crc = (crc >> 1) ^ (0xEDB88320 & -(crc & 1));
        }
    }
    return ~crc;
}

} // namespace garuda

FILE: E:\GARUDA\git\garuda-controls\sentinel\components\garuda_core\resource_engine.cpp, encoding=UTF-8
#include "resource_engine.h"
#include <cmath>

namespace garuda {

ResourceEngine::ResourceEngine() {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < static_cast<size_t>(ResourceId::RES_COUNT); ++i) {
        records_[i].value = 0.0f;
        records_[i].quality = QualityTag::UNAVAILABLE;
        records_[i].source_timestamp = 0;
        records_[i].receive_timestamp = 0;
        records_[i].has_valid_reading = false;
    }
}

float ResourceEngine::convert_to_canonical(float raw_val, UnitType unit) const {
    switch (unit) {
        case UnitType::CELSIUS:
            return raw_val + 273.15f;
        case UnitType::FAHRENHEIT:
            return (raw_val - 32.0f) * (5.0f / 9.0f) + 273.15f;
        case UnitType::KELVIN:
        case UnitType::PERCENT:
        case UnitType::AMPERES:
        case UnitType::VOLTS:
        default:
            return raw_val; // Base SI units are canonical
    }
}

void ResourceEngine::ingest(ResourceId id, float raw_val, UnitType unit, QualityTag quality) {
    size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(id);
    if (index >= static_cast<size_t>(ResourceId::RES_COUNT)) return;

    monotonic_time_t now = monotonic_micros();
    ResourceRecord &rec = records_[index];

    rec.receive_timestamp = now;

    if (std::isnan(raw_val) || quality == QualityTag::INVALID) {
        rec.quality = QualityTag::INVALID;
        return;
    }

    rec.value = convert_to_canonical(raw_val, unit);
    rec.quality = quality;
    rec.source_timestamp = now;
    rec.has_valid_reading = true;
}

void ResourceEngine::update_freshness() {
    monotonic_time_t now = monotonic_micros();

    for (size_t i = 0; i < static_cast<size_t>(ResourceId::RES_COUNT); ++i) {
        ResourceRecord &rec = records_[i];

        if (!rec.has_valid_reading) {
            rec.quality = QualityTag::UNAVAILABLE;
            continue;
        }

        uint64_t age_us = (now >= rec.source_timestamp) ? (now - rec.source_timestamp) : 0;

        if (age_us > EXPIRATION_LIMIT_US) {
            rec.quality = QualityTag::TIMEOUT;
        } else if (age_us > FRESHNESS_LIMIT_US) {
            rec.quality = QualityTag::STALE;
        } else {
            rec.quality = QualityTag::GOOD;
        }
    }
}

const ResourceRecord &ResourceEngine::get_record(ResourceId id) const {
    return records_[static_cast<size_t>(id)];
}

float ResourceEngine::get_canonical_value(ResourceId id) const {
    return records_[static_cast<size_t>(id)].value;
}

QualityTag ResourceEngine::get_quality(ResourceId id) const {
    return records_[static_cast<size_t>(id)].quality;
}

bool ResourceEngine::is_usable(ResourceId id) const {
    QualityTag q = records_[static_cast<size_t>(id)].quality;
    return (q == QualityTag::GOOD || q == QualityTag::STALE);
}

float ResourceEngine::get_as_celsius(ResourceId id) const {
    return records_[static_cast<size_t>(id)].value - 273.15f;
}

float ResourceEngine::get_as_fahrenheit(ResourceId id) const {
    return (records_[static_cast<size_t>(id)].value - 273.15f) * 1.8f + 32.0f;
}

} // namespace garuda

FILE: E:\GARUDA\git\garuda-controls\sentinel\components\garuda_core\resource_engine.h, encoding=UTF-8
#pragma once

#include "garuda_types.h"
#include "monotonic_clock.h"
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstddef>

namespace garuda {

// Canonical data record for a single logical resource
struct ResourceRecord {
    float value{0.0f};                      // Stored in canonical units (Kelvin for temp, % for hum)
    QualityTag quality{QualityTag::UNAVAILABLE};
    monotonic_time_t source_timestamp{0};   // Observation creation timestamp (GLOB-34)
    monotonic_time_t receive_timestamp{0};  // Local receipt timestamp (GLOB-44)
    bool has_valid_reading{false};
};

class ResourceEngine {
public:
    ResourceEngine();

    // Ingests raw data, converts to canonical units, and tags timestamps
    void ingest(ResourceId id, float raw_val, UnitType unit, QualityTag quality = QualityTag::GOOD);

    // Evaluates data freshness across all resources (called periodically in main loop)
    void update_freshness();

    // Query APIs
    const ResourceRecord &get_record(ResourceId id) const;
    float get_canonical_value(ResourceId id) const;
    QualityTag get_quality(ResourceId id) const;
    bool is_usable(ResourceId id) const;

    // Unit conversion helpers
    float get_as_celsius(ResourceId id) const;
    float get_as_fahrenheit(ResourceId id) const;

private:
    static constexpr uint64_t FRESHNESS_LIMIT_US = 5000000ULL;  // 5.0 seconds (GLOB-43)
    static constexpr uint64_t EXPIRATION_LIMIT_US = 30000000ULL; // 30.0 seconds (GLOB-43)

    float convert_to_canonical(float raw_val, UnitType unit) const;

    ResourceRecord records_[static_cast<size_t>(ResourceId::RES_COUNT)];
};

} // namespace garuda

FILE: E:\GARUDA\git\garuda-controls\sentinel\components\garuda_core\rotary_encoder.cpp, encoding=UTF-8
#include "rotary_encoder.h"

namespace garuda {

RotaryEncoder::RotaryEncoder() {}

EncoderEvent RotaryEncoder::update(bool pin_a, bool pin_b, bool pin_btn) {
    EncoderEvent event = EncoderEvent::NONE;

    // 1. Mathematically Pure 16-State Quadrature Transition Matrix
    // Index: (Old_A << 3) | (Old_B << 2) | (New_A << 1) | (New_B)
    // +1 = Clockwise edge, -1 = Counter-Clockwise edge, 0 = Illegal/Neutral
    static const int8_t QUAD_TABLE[16] = {
         0,  1, -1,  0,  // from 00: 00(0), 01(+1), 10(-1), 11(0)
        -1,  0,  0,  1,  // from 01: 00(-1), 01(0), 10(0),  11(+1)
         1,  0,  0, -1,  // from 10: 00(+1), 01(0), 10(0),  11(-1)
         0, -1,  1,  0   // from 11: 00(0), 01(-1), 10(+1), 11(0)
    };

    uint8_t current_pins = ((pin_a ? 1 : 0) << 1) | (pin_b ? 1 : 0);
    uint8_t last_pins = ((last_a_ ? 1 : 0) << 1) | (last_b_ ? 1 : 0);

    if (current_pins != last_pins) {
        state_ = ((last_pins << 2) | current_pins) & 0x0F;
        int8_t step = QUAD_TABLE[state_];

        last_a_ = pin_a;
        last_b_ = pin_b;

        if (step != 0) {
            accumulator_ += step;
            // EC11 detent cycle: fires exactly 1 clean event per physical detent click
            if (accumulator_ >= 2) {
                event = EncoderEvent::CLOCKWISE;
                accumulator_ = 0;
            } else if (accumulator_ <= -2) {
                event = EncoderEvent::ANTICLOCKWISE;
                accumulator_ = 0;
            }
        }
    }

    // 2. Active-Low Pushbutton with 20ms Debounce & 1s Long-Press
    bool raw_pressed = !pin_btn;

    if (raw_pressed != btn_state_) {
        if (btn_debounce_timer_.has_expired()) {
            btn_state_ = raw_pressed;
            btn_debounce_timer_.reset();

            if (btn_state_) {
                long_press_timer_.reset();
                btn_latched_ = false;
            } else {
                if (!btn_latched_) {
                    event = EncoderEvent::CLICK;
                }
            }
        }
    } else if (btn_state_ && !btn_latched_) {
        if (long_press_timer_.has_expired()) {
            btn_latched_ = true;
            event = EncoderEvent::LONG_PRESS;
        }
    }

    return event;
}

} // namespace garuda

FILE: E:\GARUDA\git\garuda-controls\sentinel\components\garuda_core\rotary_encoder.h, encoding=UTF-8
#pragma once

#include "monotonic_clock.h"
#include <cstdint>

namespace garuda {

enum class EncoderEvent : uint8_t {
    NONE = 0,
    CLOCKWISE,
    ANTICLOCKWISE,
    CLICK,
    LONG_PRESS
};

class RotaryEncoder {
public:
    RotaryEncoder();

    // Call this periodically or on pin interrupts
    EncoderEvent update(bool pin_a, bool pin_b, bool pin_btn);

    bool is_button_down() const { return btn_state_; }

private:
    uint8_t state_{0};
    int8_t accumulator_{0};
    bool last_a_{true};
    bool last_b_{true};
    bool btn_state_{false};
    bool btn_latched_{false};

    ElapsedTimer btn_debounce_timer_{20000ULL};    // 20ms debounce
    ElapsedTimer long_press_timer_{1000000ULL};    // 1000ms long press
};

FILE: E:\GARUDA\git\garuda-controls\sentinel\components\garuda_core\servo_driver.cpp, encoding=UTF-8
#include "servo_driver.h"
#include "esphome/core/log.h"
#include <algorithm>

static const char *const TAG = "garuda.servo";

namespace garuda {

ServoDriver::ServoDriver() {}

void ServoDriver::init(const ServoConfig &config) {
    config_ = config;

    step_timer_.set_interval_millis(config_.step_delay_ms);
    settle_timer_.set_interval_millis(config_.settle_time_ms);

    current_pos_pct_ = 0.0f;
    target_pos_pct_ = 0.0f;
    state_ = FlapMovementState::CLOSED;
    is_attached_ = false;

    if (output_ != nullptr) {
        output_->set_level(0.0f);
    }

    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Servo Driver initialized via FloatOutput HAL");
}

void ServoDriver::update_config(const ServoConfig &config) {
    config_ = config;
    step_timer_.set_interval_millis(config_.step_delay_ms);
    settle_timer_.set_interval_millis(config_.settle_time_ms);
}

void ServoDriver::write_pulse_us(uint32_t us) {
    if (output_ == nullptr) return;
    // 50Hz = 20,000us period. Duty = pulse_width / 20,000us
    us = std::clamp(us, config_.min_us, config_.max_us);
    float duty = static_cast<float>(us) / 20000.0f;
    output_->set_level(std::clamp(duty, 0.0f, 0.15f));
}

void ServoDriver::attach_hardware() {
    is_attached_ = true;
}

void ServoDriver::detach_hardware() {
    if (output_ != nullptr && is_attached_) {
        output_->set_level(0.0f);
        is_attached_ = false;
        ESP_LOGD(TAG, "Servo coils detached for power dissipation & wear prevention (FLP-03).");
    }
}

void ServoDriver::force_seat_closed() {
    attach_hardware();
    current_pos_pct_ = 0.0f;
    target_pos_pct_ = 0.0f;
    state_ = FlapMovementState::SETTLING;
    settle_timer_.reset();

    float factor = config_.invert ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
    uint32_t pulse = config_.min_us + static_cast<uint32_t>(factor * (config_.max_us - config_.min_us));
    write_pulse_us(pulse);
    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Damper seat pulse applied at boot. Settling 5.0s before detach.");
}

void ServoDriver::emergency_detach() {
    detach_hardware();
    state_ = FlapMovementState::CLOSED;
    ESP_LOGW(TAG, "EMERGENCY DETACH: Servo coils unpowered at current position (OM-04).");
}

void ServoDriver::set_target(percent_t target_pct) {
    target_pct = std::clamp(target_pct, 0.0f, 100.0f);
    if (std::abs(target_pos_pct_ - target_pct) < 0.1f && is_attached_) return;

    target_pos_pct_ = target_pct;
    attach_hardware();
    settle_timer_.reset();
}

void ServoDriver::process() {
    // 1. If target position reached, handle steady holding & settling countdown
    if (std::abs(current_pos_pct_ - target_pos_pct_) <= 0.1f) {
        current_pos_pct_ = target_pos_pct_;

        if (state_ == FlapMovementState::OPENING || state_ == FlapMovementState::CLOSING) {
            state_ = FlapMovementState::SETTLING;
            settle_timer_.reset();
        } else if (state_ == FlapMovementState::SETTLING) {
            if (settle_timer_.has_expired()) {
                detach_hardware();
                state_ = (current_pos_pct_ <= 0.5f) ? FlapMovementState::CLOSED : FlapMovementState::OPEN;
            } else {
                // Keep writing steady holding pulse while settling
                float factor = std::clamp(current_pos_pct_ / 100.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f);
                if (config_.invert) factor = 1.0f - factor;
                uint32_t pulse = config_.min_us + static_cast<uint32_t>(factor * (config_.max_us - config_.min_us));
                write_pulse_us(pulse);
            }
        }
        return;
    }

    // 2. Step profiler (FLP-03: Slew rate rate limiting)
    if (step_timer_.has_expired_and_reset()) {
        attach_hardware();

        if (current_pos_pct_ < target_pos_pct_) {
            state_ = FlapMovementState::OPENING;
            current_pos_pct_ += config_.step_size_pct;
            if (current_pos_pct_ > target_pos_pct_) current_pos_pct_ = target_pos_pct_;
        } else {
            state_ = FlapMovementState::CLOSING;
            current_pos_pct_ -= config_.step_size_pct;
            if (current_pos_pct_ < target_pos_pct_) current_pos_pct_ = target_pos_pct_;
        }

        float factor = std::clamp(current_pos_pct_ / 100.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f);
        if (config_.invert) factor = 1.0f - factor;

        uint32_t pulse = config_.min_us + static_cast<uint32_t>(factor * (config_.max_us - config_.min_us));
        write_pulse_us(pulse);
    }
}

} // namespace garuda

#pragma once

#include "garuda_types.h"
#include "monotonic_clock.h"
#include "esphome/components/output/float_output.h"

namespace garuda {

struct ServoConfig {
    uint32_t min_us{1000};         // Pulse for 0%
    uint32_t max_us{2000};         // Pulse for 100%
    bool invert{false};            // Directional inversion (FLP-01)
    uint32_t step_delay_ms{50};    // Slew interval
    float step_size_pct{1.0f};     // Slew increment
    uint32_t settle_time_ms{5000}; // Stationary coil detach delay (FLP-03)
};

class ServoDriver {
public:
    ServoDriver();

    void set_output(esphome::output::FloatOutput *output) { output_ = output; }
    void init(const ServoConfig &config);
    void update_config(const ServoConfig &config);

    // Forces physical damper seating at boot before coil detachment
    void force_seat_closed();

    // Commands normalized logical target (0.0% to 100.0%)
    void set_target(percent_t target_pct);

    // Core periodic stepper (called in loop)
    void process();

    // Emergency safe shutdown: immediately detaches coils (OM-04)
    void emergency_detach();

    // State inspections
    percent_t get_current_position() const { return current_pos_pct_; }
    percent_t get_target_position() const { return target_pos_pct_; }
    FlapMovementState get_movement_state() const { return state_; }
    bool is_attached() const { return is_attached_; }

private:
    void write_pulse_us(uint32_t us);
    void attach_hardware();
    void detach_hardware();

    esphome::output::FloatOutput *output_{nullptr};
    ServoConfig config_;
    percent_t current_pos_pct_{0.0f};
    percent_t target_pos_pct_{0.0f};
    FlapMovementState state_{FlapMovementState::CLOSED};

    bool is_attached_{false};
    ElapsedTimer step_timer_{50000ULL};
    ElapsedTimer settle_timer_{5000000ULL};
};

    fan_control_.init(fcfg);

    // 3. Restore persisted safety latches (GLOB-26, FHT-02)
    if (active_config_.fan_stall_latched) {
        fan_driver_.latch_stall();
        health_state_ = SystemHealthState::MOTOR_STALL;
        ESP_LOGE("SAFETY", "CRITICAL: Restored persisted MOTOR STALL safety latch!");
    } else {
        health_state_ = SystemHealthState::OK;
    }

    // 4. Initialize Local Hardware UI Controller (Stage 8)
    ui_ = std::make_unique<EqUiController>(*this);

    esp_reset_reason_t reason = esp_reset_reason();
    ESP_LOGI("SYSTEM", "Hardware Reset Reason Code: %d", static_cast<int>(reason));

    telemetry_timer_.reset();
    climate_eval_timer_.reset();
    tick_timer_.reset();
    ESP_LOGI("SYSTEM", "==================================================");
}

void EqApp::feed_encoder_pins(bool pin_a, bool pin_b, bool pin_btn) {
    EncoderEvent ev = encoder_.update(pin_a, pin_b, pin_btn);
    if (ui_ != nullptr) {
        ui_->process_encoder_event(ev);
    }
}

const char *EqApp::get_display_line(uint8_t line_idx) const {
    if (ui_ != nullptr) {
        return ui_->get_display_line(line_idx);
    }
    return "";
}

EqSafetyContext EqApp::build_safety_context() const {
    EqSafetyContext ctx;
    ctx.emergency_stop_active        = emergency_stop_active_;
    ctx.maintenance_lockout_active   = active_config_.maintenance_lockout;
    ctx.fan_stall_latched            = fan_control_.is_stalled();
    ctx.fan_is_spinning              = (fan_control_.get_current_speed() > 0.0f || 
                                        fan_control_.get_rpm() > 50.0f || 
                                        fan_driver_.is_relay_energized());
    ctx.flap_capability_enabled      = active_config_.flap_enabled;
    ctx.current_flap_position        = flap_control_.get_current_position();
    ctx.min_safe_flap_opening        = active_config_.min_safe_flap_opening_pct;
    return ctx;
}

bool EqApp::is_vent_active() const {
    if (active_config_.operational_mode == 0) return false;
    if (vent_power_active_) return true;
    return (fan_control_.get_target_speed() > 0.0f || 
            flap_control_.get_target_position() >= active_config_.min_safe_flap_opening_pct);
}

void EqApp::set_vent_power(bool on) {
    vent_power_active_ = on;

    if (on) {
        if (active_config_.operational_mode == 0) {
            active_config_.operational_mode = 3;
        }

        percent_t target_flap = flap_control_.calculate_curve_position(active_config_.manual_fan_speed, active_config_);
        ESP_LOGI("VENT", "Ventilation ON -> Target Flap %.0f%%, Fan to %.0f%%", target_flap, active_config_.manual_fan_speed);
        flap_control_.command_target(target_flap, emergency_stop_active_, active_config_.maintenance_lockout);
        pending_fan_target_ = active_config_.manual_fan_speed;

        if (flap_control_.get_current_position() >= active_config_.min_safe_flap_opening_pct) {
            EqSafetyContext ctx = build_safety_context();
            OmResult om = EqOmTables::evaluate_fan_run(ctx);
            if (om.is_permitted()) {
                fan_control_.command_speed(active_config_.manual_fan_speed, emergency_stop_active_, active_config_.maintenance_lockout);
                pending_fan_target_ = 0.0f;
            }
        }
    } else {
        ESP_LOGI("VENT", "Ventilation OFF -> Halting fan, sealing flap to 0%%");
        pending_fan_target_ = 0.0f;
        set_manual_flap_open(false);
    }
}

void EqApp::set_fan_run_speed(percent_t speed_pct) {
    speed_pct = std::clamp(speed_pct, 10.0f, 100.0f);
    
    if (std::abs(active_config_.manual_fan_speed - speed_pct) > 0.1f) {
        active_config_.manual_fan_speed = speed_pct;
        config_dirty_ = true;
        save_debounce_timer_.reset();
        ESP_LOGI("VENT", "Stored Fan Running Speed set to: %.0f%%", speed_pct);
    }

    if (vent_power_active_ && active_config_.operational_mode == 3 && 
        flap_control_.get_current_position() >= active_config_.min_safe_flap_opening_pct) {
        percent_t target_flap = flap_control_.calculate_curve_position(speed_pct, active_config_);
        flap_control_.command_target(target_flap, emergency_stop_active_, active_config_.maintenance_lockout);
        fan_control_.command_speed(speed_pct, emergency_stop_active_, active_config_.maintenance_lockout);
        return;
    }

    if (!vent_power_active_) {
        set_vent_power(true);
    }
}

void EqApp::set_operational_mode(uint8_t mode) {
    if (manual_close_takeover_active_) {
        pre_takeover_mode_ = mode;
        ESP_LOGI("MODE", "Takeover active: Updated recovery mode to: %u", mode);
        return;
    }

    if (active_config_.operational_mode != mode) {
        active_config_.operational_mode = mode;
        config_dirty_ = true;
        save_debounce_timer_.reset();
        climate_engine_.reset_fallback();

        if (mode == 0) {
            set_vent_power(false);
        }

        ESP_LOGI("MODE", "Operating Mode set to: %s (%u)", get_mode_string(), mode);
    }
}

const char *EqApp::get_mode_string() const {
    switch (active_config_.operational_mode) {
        case 0: return "OFF";
        case 1: return "AUTO/TEMP";
        case 2: return "AUTO/HUM";
        case 3: return "MANUAL";
        default: return "UNKNOWN";
    }
}

void EqApp::update_desired_temperature(kelvin_t temp_k) {
    if (std::abs(active_config_.desired_temp_k - temp_k) > 0.01f) {
        active_config_.desired_temp_k = temp_k;
        active_config_.operational_mode = 1;
        config_dirty_ = true;
        save_debounce_timer_.reset();
        ESP_LOGI("CLIMATE", "Target Temp updated: %.2f K (%.1f C) -> Switched to AUTO/TEMP",
                 temp_k, temp_k - 273.15f);
    }
}

void EqApp::update_desired_humidity(percent_t hum_pct) {
    if (std::abs(active_config_.desired_humidity_pct - hum_pct) > 0.1f) {
        active_config_.desired_humidity_pct = hum_pct;
        active_config_.operational_mode = 2;
        config_dirty_ = true;
        save_debounce_timer_.reset();
        ESP_LOGI("CLIMATE", "Target Hum updated: %.1f%% -> Switched to AUTO/HUM", hum_pct);
    }
}

void EqApp::set_response_mode(uint8_t mode) {
    if (active_config_.response_mode != mode) {
        active_config_.response_mode = mode;
        config_dirty_ = true;
        save_debounce_timer_.reset();
        ESP_LOGI("CLIMATE", "Response Mode set to: %u (0:Manual, 1:Quiet, 2:Perf)", mode);
    }
}

void EqApp::set_manual_fan_speed(percent_t speed_pct) {
    if (active_config_.operational_mode != 3) {
        active_config_.operational_mode = 3;
    }

    set_fan_run_speed(speed_pct > 0.0f ? speed_pct : active_config_.manual_fan_speed);

    if (speed_pct <= 0.0f) {
        set_vent_power(false);
        return;
    }

    set_vent_power(true);
}

void EqApp::set_manual_flap_open(bool open) {
    EqSafetyContext ctx = build_safety_context();

    if (!open) {
        vent_power_active_ = false;

        if (ctx.fan_is_spinning) {
            if (!manual_close_takeover_active_) {
                pre_takeover_mode_ = active_config_.operational_mode;
                ESP_LOGW("FLAP", "Takeover Engaged: Fan is spinning. Saved mode %s (%u) to RAM (FFI-03, FLAP-022)",
                         get_mode_string(), pre_takeover_mode_);
            }

            manual_close_takeover_active_ = true;
            takeover_timeout_timer_.reset();
            pending_fan_target_ = 0.0f;

            fan_control_.command_speed(0.0f, emergency_stop_active_, active_config_.maintenance_lockout);
            return;
        }

        manual_close_takeover_active_ = false;
        active_config_.manual_flap_target = 0.0f;
        config_dirty_ = true;
        save_debounce_timer_.reset();
        flap_control_.command_target(0.0f, emergency_stop_active_, active_config_.maintenance_lockout);
        ESP_LOGI("FLAP", "Flap commanded OFF: moving from %.1f%% to 0%%", flap_control_.get_current_position());
    } else {
        if (manual_close_takeover_active_) {
            manual_close_takeover_active_ = false;
            ESP_LOGI("FLAP", "Manual close takeover cancelled. Restored previous Operating Mode from RAM: %s (%u) (FLAP-022)",
                     get_mode_string(), pre_takeover_mode_);
        }

        active_config_.manual_flap_target = 100.0f;
        config_dirty_ = true;
        save_debounce_timer_.reset();
        flap_control_.command_target(100.0f, emergency_stop_active_, active_config_.maintenance_lockout);
        ESP_LOGI("FLAP", "Flap commanded ON: moving from %.1f%% to 100%%", flap_control_.get_current_position());
    }
}

void EqApp::execute_climate_loop() {
    if (active_config_.operational_mode != 1 && active_config_.operational_mode != 2) {
        return;
    }

    ClimateCalculationResult res = climate_engine_.evaluate(resources_, active_config_);
    last_climate_reason_ = res.reason;
    percent_t target_fan = res.effective_target;

    // Handle 5-minute fallback timeout (AUT-04)
    if (res.fallback_timed_out) {
        ESP_LOGE("CLIMATE", "Sensor fallback expired (>5min). Transferring authority to MANUAL at %.0f%% (AUT-04)", target_fan);
        active_config_.operational_mode = 3;
        active_config_.manual_fan_speed = target_fan;
        config_dirty_ = true;
        save_debounce_timer_.reset();
    }

    if (target_fan > 0.0f) {
        percent_t required_flap = flap_control_.calculate_curve_position(target_fan, active_config_);

        if (flap_control_.get_current_position() < active_config_.min_safe_flap_opening_pct) {
            pending_fan_target_ = target_fan;
            flap_control_.command_target(required_flap, emergency_stop_active_, active_config_.maintenance_lockout);
            return;
        }

        flap_control_.command_target(required_flap, emergency_stop_active_, active_config_.maintenance_lockout);
        
        EqSafetyContext ctx = build_safety_context();
        OmResult om = EqOmTables::evaluate_fan_run(ctx);
        if (om.is_permitted()) {
            pending_fan_target_ = 0.0f;
            fan_control_.command_speed(target_fan, emergency_stop_active_, active_config_.maintenance_lockout);
        }
    } else {
        pending_fan_target_ = 0.0f;
        fan_control_.command_speed(0.0f, emergency_stop_active_, active_config_.maintenance_lockout);

        if (!fan_control_.is_running()) {
            flap_control_.command_target(0.0f, emergency_stop_active_, active_config_.maintenance_lockout);
        }
    }
}

void EqApp::execute_coordination() {
    EqSafetyContext ctx = build_safety_context();

    if (manual_close_takeover_active_) {
        bool fan_stopped = (!ctx.fan_is_spinning && !fan_driver_.is_relay_energized());
        bool timeout_expired = takeover_timeout_timer_.has_expired();

        if (fan_stopped || timeout_expired) {
            if (timeout_expired && ctx.fan_is_spinning) {
                ESP_LOGW("FLAP", "Spin-down timeout (15s) elapsed. Forcing flap to 0%% (CLOSED)");
            } else {
                ESP_LOGI("FLAP", "Fan confirmed halted and relay isolated. Sealing flap to 0%% (CLOSED)");
            }
            flap_control_.command_target(0.0f, emergency_stop_active_, active_config_.maintenance_lockout);
            manual_close_takeover_active_ = false;
        }
        return;
    }

    if (pending_fan_target_ > 0.0f) {
        if (flap_control_.get_current_position() >= active_config_.min_safe_flap_opening_pct) {
            OmResult result = EqOmTables::evaluate_fan_run(ctx);
            if (result.is_permitted()) {
                ESP_LOGI("FAN", "Flap satisfies required opening. Releasing fan ramp to %.1f%%", pending_fan_target_);
                fan_control_.command_speed(pending_fan_target_, emergency_stop_active_, active_config_.maintenance_lockout);
                pending_fan_target_ = 0.0f;
            }
        }
    }
}

void EqApp::set_maintenance_lockout(bool locked) {
    if (active_config_.maintenance_lockout != locked) {
        active_config_.maintenance_lockout = locked;
        config_dirty_ = true;
        save_debounce_timer_.reset();

        if (locked) {
            ESP_LOGW("SAFETY", "Maintenance Lockout ENGAGED. Cutting all actuators.");
            fan_control_.command_speed(0.0f, emergency_stop_active_, true);
        } else {
            ESP_LOGI("SAFETY", "Maintenance Lockout RELEASED.");
        }
    }
}

void EqApp::trigger_emergency_stop() {
    emergency_stop_active_ = true;
    fan_control_.command_speed(0.0f, true, true);
    flap_control_.command_target(flap_control_.get_current_position(), true, true);
    ESP_LOGE("SAFETY", "EMERGENCY STOP TRIGGERED!");
}

void EqApp::reset_emergency_stop() {
    emergency_stop_active_ = false;
    ESP_LOGI("SAFETY", "Emergency Stop reset.");
}

void EqApp::clear_faults() {
    fan_control_.clear_fault();
    manual_close_takeover_active_ = false;
    if (active_config_.fan_stall_latched) {
        active_config_.fan_stall_latched = false;
        commit_configuration();
    }
    ESP_LOGI("SAFETY", "Faults and latches cleared.");
}

void EqApp::set_flap_enabled(bool enabled) {
    if (active_config_.flap_enabled != enabled) {
        active_config_.flap_enabled = enabled;
        config_dirty_ = true;
        save_debounce_timer_.reset();
        ESP_LOGI("FLAP", "Flap capability set to: %s", enabled ? "ENABLED" : "BYPASS");
    }
}

void EqApp::set_flap_inversion(bool inverted) {
    if (active_config_.flap_invert != inverted) {
        active_config_.flap_invert = inverted;
        config_dirty_ = true;
        save_debounce_timer_.reset();

        ServoConfig cfg;
        cfg.min_us         = active_config_.flap_min_us;
        cfg.max_us         = active_config_.flap_max_us;
        cfg.invert         = inverted;
        cfg.step_delay_ms  = active_config_.flap_step_delay_ms;
        cfg.step_size_pct  = active_config_.flap_step_size_pct;
        cfg.settle_time_ms = active_config_.flap_settle_time_ms;
        servo_driver_.update_config(cfg);
        ESP_LOGI("FLAP", "Inversion set to: %s", inverted ? "TRUE" : "FALSE");
    }
}

void EqApp::set_tach_enabled(bool enabled) {
    if (active_config_.tach_enabled != enabled) {
        active_config_.tach_enabled = enabled;
        config_dirty_ = true;
        save_debounce_timer_.reset();

        FanConfig cfg;
        cfg.pwm_min                = active_config_.fan_pwm_min;
        cfg.pwm_max                = active_config_.fan_pwm_max;
        cfg.slew_rate_pct_per_sec  = active_config_.fan_slew_rate_pct_per_sec;
        cfg.tach_enabled           = enabled;
        cfg.stall_rpm_threshold    = active_config_.fan_stall_rpm_threshold;
        fan_driver_.update_config(cfg);

        if (!enabled) clear_faults();
        ESP_LOGI("FAN", "Tachometer feedback set to: %s", enabled ? "ENABLED" : "DISABLED");
    }
}

const char *EqApp::get_fan_state_string() const {
    switch (fan_control_.get_state()) {
        case FanRunState::STOPPED:           return "STOPPED";
        case FanRunState::RELAY_SETTLING:    return "RELAY_SETTLE";
        case FanRunState::STARTING_BOOST:    return "BOOST";
        case FanRunState::RAMPING:           return "RAMP";
        case FanRunState::RUNNING:           return "RUN";
        case FanRunState::STOPPING_SLEW:     return "STOP_SLEW";
        case FanRunState::STOPPING_SPINDOWN: return "SPINDOWN";
        case FanRunState::SHUTDOWN_DWELL:    return "DWELL_10S";
        case FanRunState::STALLED:           return "STALLED";
        default:                             return "UNKNOWN";
    }
}

const char *EqApp::get_flap_state_string() const {
    switch (flap_control_.get_movement_state()) {
        case FlapMovementState::CLOSED:   return "CLOSED";
        case FlapMovementState::OPENING:  return "OPENING";
        case FlapMovementState::OPEN:     return "OPEN";
        case FlapMovementState::CLOSING:  return "CLOSING";
        case FlapMovementState::SETTLING: return "SETTLE";
        default:                          return "UNKNOWN";
    }
}

void EqApp::ingest_internal_temp(float val_celsius) {
    resources_.ingest(ResourceId::RES_INTERNAL_TEMP, val_celsius, UnitType::CELSIUS);
}

void EqApp::ingest_internal_hum(float val_pct) {
    resources_.ingest(ResourceId::RES_INTERNAL_HUM, val_pct, UnitType::PERCENT);
}

void EqApp::ingest_external_temp(float val_celsius) {
    resources_.ingest(ResourceId::RES_EXTERNAL_TEMP, val_celsius, UnitType::CELSIUS);
}

void EqApp::ingest_external_hum(float val_pct) {
    resources_.ingest(ResourceId::RES_EXTERNAL_HUM, val_pct, UnitType::PERCENT);
}

void EqApp::ingest_fan_rpm(float rpm) {
    fan_control_.ingest_rpm(rpm);
}

void EqApp::ingest_fan_current(float amps) {
    resources_.ingest(ResourceId::RES_FAN_CURRENT, amps, UnitType::AMPERES);
    fan_control_.ingest_current(amps);
}

void EqApp::load_factory_defaults() {
    active_config_ = EqConfig();
}

void EqApp::load_configuration() {
    EqConfig loaded;
    size_t payload_len = sizeof(EqConfig) - sizeof(uint32_t);

    if (PersistenceManager::load_blob(NVS_NAMESPACE, NVS_KEY_CONFIG, &loaded, sizeof(EqConfig))) {
        uint32_t computed_crc = PersistenceManager::calculate_crc32(reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t *>(&loaded), payload_len);
        if (computed_crc == loaded.crc32) {
            active_config_ = loaded;
            return;
        }
    }
    load_factory_defaults();
    commit_configuration();
}

void EqApp::commit_configuration() {
    size_t payload_len = sizeof(EqConfig) - sizeof(uint32_t);
    active_config_.crc32 = PersistenceManager::calculate_crc32(
        reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t *>(&active_config_), payload_len);

    PersistenceManager::save_blob(NVS_NAMESPACE, NVS_KEY_CONFIG, &active_config_, sizeof(EqConfig));
    config_dirty_ = false;
}

void EqApp::print_status_line() {
    char line_buf[384];
    size_t offset = 0;
    bool has_prev = false;

    auto append_sep = [&]() {
        if (has_prev && offset < sizeof(line_buf)) {
            offset += snprintf(line_buf + offset, sizeof(line_buf) - offset, " || ");
        }
        has_prev = true;
    };

    auto q_str = [](QualityTag q) -> const char* {
        switch (q) {
            case QualityTag::GOOD: return "GOOD";
            case QualityTag::STALE: return "STALE";
            case QualityTag::TIMEOUT: return "TIMEOUT";
            default: return "UNAVAIL";
        }
    };

    // 1. Internal Environment
    if (telemetry_mask_ & LOG_INT) {
        append_sep();
        float t = resources_.get_as_celsius(ResourceId::RES_INTERNAL_TEMP);
        float h = resources_.get_canonical_value(ResourceId::RES_INTERNAL_HUM);
        offset += snprintf(line_buf + offset, sizeof(line_buf) - offset,
                           "INT: %.1fC %.0f%% [%s]", t, h, q_str(resources_.get_quality(ResourceId::RES_INTERNAL_TEMP)));
    }

    // 2. External Environment
    if (telemetry_mask_ & LOG_EXT) {
        append_sep();
        float t = resources_.get_as_celsius(ResourceId::RES_EXTERNAL_TEMP);
        float h = resources_.get_canonical_value(ResourceId::RES_EXTERNAL_HUM);
        offset += snprintf(line_buf + offset, sizeof(line_buf) - offset,
                           "EXT: %.1fC %.0f%% [%s]", t, h, q_str(resources_.get_quality(ResourceId::RES_EXTERNAL_TEMP)));
    }

    // 3. Operational Mode & Target Setpoint
    append_sep();
    if (active_config_.operational_mode == 1) {
        offset += snprintf(line_buf + offset, sizeof(line_buf) - offset,
                           "MODE: AUTO/T (%.1fC)", active_config_.desired_temp_k - 273.15f);
    } else if (active_config_.operational_mode == 2) {
        offset += snprintf(line_buf + offset, sizeof(line_buf) - offset,
                           "MODE: AUTO/H (%.0f%%)", active_config_.desired_humidity_pct);
    } else {
        offset += snprintf(line_buf + offset, sizeof(line_buf) - offset,
                           "MODE: %s", get_mode_string());
    }

    // 4. Fan Actuator Status
    if (telemetry_mask_ & LOG_FAN) {
        append_sep();
        offset += snprintf(line_buf + offset, sizeof(line_buf) - offset,
                           "FAN: %.0f%% (%.0f RPM, %.2fA, Relay: %s) [%s]",
                           fan_control_.get_current_speed(),
                           fan_control_.get_rpm(),
                           fan_control_.get_current_amps(),
                           fan_driver_.is_relay_energized() ? "ON" : "OFF",
                           get_fan_state_string());
    }

    // 5. Flap Actuator Status
    if (telemetry_mask_ & LOG_FLAP) {
        append_sep();
        offset += snprintf(line_buf + offset, sizeof(line_buf) - offset,
                           "FLAP: %.0f%% [%s]",
                           flap_control_.get_current_position(),
                           get_flap_state_string());
    }

    // 6. Operational Matrix Gating
    if (telemetry_mask_ & LOG_OM) {
        append_sep();
        EqSafetyContext ctx = build_safety_context();
        OmResult om = EqOmTables::evaluate_fan_run(ctx);
        
        auto reason_str = [](ReasonCode r) -> const char* {
            switch (r) {
                case ReasonCode::OK: return "OK";
                case ReasonCode::ESTOP_ACTIVE: return "ESTOP";
                case ReasonCode::LOCKOUT_ACTIVE: return "LOCKOUT";
                case ReasonCode::MOTOR_STALL: return "STALL";
                case ReasonCode::FLAP_INTERLOCK_BLOCKED: return "FLAP_CLOSED";
                case ReasonCode::TACH_DISAGREEMENT: return "TACH_FAULT";
                case ReasonCode::INTERNAL_SENSOR_FAULT: return "SENSOR_FAULT";
                default: return "UNKNOWN";
            }
        };

        if (om.is_permitted()) {
            offset += snprintf(line_buf + offset, sizeof(line_buf) - offset, "OM: PERMITTED");
        } else {
            offset += snprintf(line_buf + offset, sizeof(line_buf) - offset, "OM: PROHIBITED [%s]", reason_str(om.reason));
        }
    }

    // 7. System Health & Monotonic Uptime
    if (telemetry_mask_ & LOG_SYS) {
        append_sep();
        auto health_str = [](SystemHealthState h) -> const char* {
            switch (h) {
                case SystemHealthState::OK: return "OK";
                case SystemHealthState::LOCKOUT: return "LOCKOUT";
                case SystemHealthState::MOTOR_STALL: return "STALL";
                case SystemHealthState::SENSOR_LOST: return "SENSOR_LOST";
                case SystemHealthState::HUB_OFFLINE: return "HUB_OFFLINE";
                default: return "WARN";
            }
        };
        offset += snprintf(line_buf + offset, sizeof(line_buf) - offset,
                           "SYS: %s (%lus)",
                           health_str(health_state_),
                           static_cast<unsigned long>(monotonic_seconds()));
    }

    if (offset > 0) {
        ESP_LOGI(TAG, "%s", line_buf);
    }
}

void EqApp::loop() {
    if (this != instance_) return;

    // 1. Process local UI timers (sleep & menu timeouts)
    if (ui_ != nullptr) {
        ui_->process_encoder_event(EncoderEvent::NONE);
    }

    // 2. Evaluate autonomous climate regulation loops every 2 seconds
    if (climate_eval_timer_.has_expired_and_reset()) {
        execute_climate_loop();
    }

    // 3. Process coordination handoffs (Takeover & Startup gating)
    execute_coordination();

    // 4. Step physical drivers
    flap_control_.process();
    fan_control_.process();

    // 5. Update sensor freshness
    resources_.update_freshness();

    // 6. Update consolidated health state & persist newly latched stalls (GLOB-26, FHT-01)
    if (emergency_stop_active_ || active_config_.maintenance_lockout) {
        health_state_ = SystemHealthState::LOCKOUT;
    } else if (fan_control_.is_stalled()) {
        health_state_ = SystemHealthState::MOTOR_STALL;
        if (!active_config_.fan_stall_latched) {
            active_config_.fan_stall_latched = true;
            commit_configuration();
        }
    } else {
        health_state_ = SystemHealthState::OK;
    }

    // 7. Debounced flash commits
    if (config_dirty_ && save_debounce_timer_.has_expired()) {
        commit_configuration();
    }

    // 8. System tick
    if (tick_timer_.has_expired_and_reset()) {
        tick_counter_++;
    }

    // 9. Single-line customizable telemetry logging
    if (telemetry_timer_.has_expired_and_reset()) {
        print_status_line();
    }
}

} // namespace eq
} // namespace garuda

enum TelemetryField : uint8_t {
    LOG_INT  = 1 << 0,
    LOG_EXT  = 1 << 1,
    LOG_FAN  = 1 << 2,
    LOG_FLAP = 1 << 3,
    LOG_OM   = 1 << 4,
    LOG_SYS  = 1 << 5
};

class EqApp : public esphome::Component {

    void loop() override;

        fan_driver_.set_relay(relay);
    }

        if (show_om)   telemetry_mask_ |= LOG_OM;
        if (show_sys)  telemetry_mask_ |= LOG_SYS;
    }

FILE: E:\GARUDA\git\garuda-controls\sentinel\nodes\eq\components\eq_app\eq_climate_engine.cpp, encoding=UTF-8
#include "eq_climate_engine.h"
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>

namespace garuda {
namespace eq {

EqClimateEngine::EqClimateEngine() {}

float EqClimateEngine::apply_floor_scaling(float raw_pct) const {
    // Product-Life Fan Target Floor Limits (AUT-03)
    if (raw_pct < 5.0f) {
        return 0.0f;
    } else if (raw_pct < 10.0f) {
        return 10.0f;
    } else {
        return std::clamp(raw_pct, 10.0f, 100.0f);
    }
}

void EqClimateEngine::reset_fallback() {
    fallback_in_progress_ = false;
    sensor_fallback_timer_.reset();
}

ClimateCalculationResult EqClimateEngine::evaluate(const ResourceEngine &resources, const EqConfig &config) {
    ClimateCalculationResult result;

    // Mode 0: OFF / Manual disengages autonomous loops
    if (config.operational_mode == 0 || config.operational_mode == 3) {
        result.calculated_target = config.manual_fan_speed;
        result.effective_target = config.manual_fan_speed;
        result.reason = ReasonCode::OK;
        return result;
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // MODE 1: TEMPERATURE AUTO (AUT-01, AUT-02, AUT-04)
    // =========================================================================
    if (config.operational_mode == 1) {
        // Check primary sensor availability (AUT-04)
        if (!resources.is_usable(ResourceId::RES_INTERNAL_TEMP) ||
            !resources.is_usable(ResourceId::RES_EXTERNAL_TEMP)) {
            
            if (!fallback_in_progress_) {
                fallback_in_progress_ = true;
                sensor_fallback_timer_.reset();
                held_target_ = result.effective_target;
            }

            if (sensor_fallback_timer_.has_expired()) {
                result.calculated_target = held_target_;
                result.effective_target = held_target_;
                result.reason = ReasonCode::INTERNAL_SENSOR_FAULT;
                result.fallback_active = true;
                result.fallback_timed_out = true; // Signal mode transition to MANUAL (AUT-04)
                return result;
            }

            // Hold target during 5-minute fallback grace period
            result.calculated_target = held_target_;
            result.effective_target = held_target_;
            result.reason = ReasonCode::SENSOR_TIMEOUT;
            result.fallback_active = true;
            return result;
        }

        fallback_in_progress_ = false;

        float t_in = resources.get_canonical_value(ResourceId::RES_INTERNAL_TEMP);   // Kelvin
        float t_ext = resources.get_canonical_value(ResourceId::RES_EXTERNAL_TEMP);  // Kelvin
        float t_target = config.desired_temp_k;                                      // Kelvin

        // Determine active response ratio by response_mode (AUT-02, MCA-01)
        float ratio = config.fan_delta;
        if (config.response_mode == 1) {
            ratio = config.quiet_ratio;
        } else if (config.response_mode == 2) {
            ratio = config.perf_ratio;
        } else {
            ratio = config.fan_delta;
        }

        // 1. Cooling Demand (Indoor > Target)
        if (t_in > t_target) {
            // Directional Utility Gating (AUT-01): Outdoor air must be cooler than indoor air
            if (t_ext >= t_in) {
                result.calculated_target = 0.0f;
                result.effective_target = 0.0f;
                result.reason = ReasonCode::OUTDOOR_NOT_USEFUL;
                return result;
            }

            // Destination Clamping (AUT-01): Clamped to max(Low Setpoint, Outdoor Temp)
            float destination = std::max(t_target, t_ext);
            float error = t_in - destination;

            // Deadband Gate (0.5 Kelvin)
            if (error <= 0.5f) {
                result.calculated_target = 0.0f;
                result.effective_target = 0.0f;
                result.reason = ReasonCode::OK;
                return result;
            }

            result.calculated_target = error * ratio;
            result.effective_target = apply_floor_scaling(result.calculated_target);
            result.reason = ReasonCode::OK;
            held_target_ = result.effective_target;
            return result;
        }
        // 2. Heating Demand (Indoor < Target)
        else if (t_in < t_target) {
            // Directional Utility Gating (AUT-01): Outdoor air must be warmer than indoor air
            if (t_ext <= t_in) {
                result.calculated_target = 0.0f;
                result.effective_target = 0.0f;
                result.reason = ReasonCode::OUTDOOR_NOT_USEFUL;
                return result;
            }

            // Destination Clamping (AUT-01): Clamped to min(High Setpoint, Outdoor Temp)
            float destination = std::min(t_target, t_ext);
            float error = destination - t_in;

            // Deadband Gate (0.5 Kelvin)
            if (error <= 0.5f) {
                result.calculated_target = 0.0f;
                result.effective_target = 0.0f;
                result.reason = ReasonCode::OK;
                return result;
            }

            result.calculated_target = error * ratio;
            result.effective_target = apply_floor_scaling(result.calculated_target);
            result.reason = ReasonCode::OK;
            held_target_ = result.effective_target;
            return result;
        } else {
            result.calculated_target = 0.0f;
            result.effective_target = 0.0f;
            result.reason = ReasonCode::OK;
            return result;
        }
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // MODE 2: HUMIDITY AUTO
    // =========================================================================
    if (config.operational_mode == 2) {
        if (!resources.is_usable(ResourceId::RES_INTERNAL_HUM) ||
            !resources.is_usable(ResourceId::RES_EXTERNAL_HUM)) {
            result.calculated_target = 0.0f;
            result.effective_target = 0.0f;
            result.reason = ReasonCode::SENSOR_TIMEOUT;
            return result;
        }

        float h_in = resources.get_canonical_value(ResourceId::RES_INTERNAL_HUM);
        float h_ext = resources.get_canonical_value(ResourceId::RES_EXTERNAL_HUM);
        float h_target = config.desired_humidity_pct;

        // Dehumidification demand (Indoor > Target)
        if (h_in > h_target) {
            if (h_ext >= h_in) {
                result.calculated_target = 0.0f;
                result.effective_target = 0.0f;
                result.reason = ReasonCode::OUTDOOR_NOT_USEFUL;
                return result;
            }

            float destination = std::max(h_target, h_ext);
            float error = h_in - destination;

            // Humidity deadband: 3.0% RH
            if (error <= 3.0f) {
                result.calculated_target = 0.0f;
                result.effective_target = 0.0f;
                result.reason = ReasonCode::OK;
                return result;
            }

            result.calculated_target = error * config.fan_delta;
            result.effective_target = apply_floor_scaling(result.calculated_target);
            result.reason = ReasonCode::OK;
            return result;
        }
    }

    result.calculated_target = 0.0f;
    result.effective_target = 0.0f;
    result.reason = ReasonCode::OK;
    return result;
}

} // namespace eq
} // namespace garuda

struct ClimateCalculationResult {
    percent_t calculated_target{0.0f};
    percent_t effective_target{0.0f}; // After deadbands and floor scaling
    ReasonCode reason{ReasonCode::OK};
    bool fallback_active{false};
    bool fallback_timed_out{false};   // Signals transition to MANUAL mode (AUT-04)
};

class EqClimateEngine {

    EqClimateEngine();

    // Evaluates current environmental conditions and computes logical fan target
    ClimateCalculationResult evaluate(const ResourceEngine &resources, const EqConfig &config);

    void reset_fallback();

private:
    float apply_floor_scaling(float raw_pct) const;

    ElapsedTimer sensor_fallback_timer_{300000000ULL}; // 5-minute fallback (AUT-04)
    bool fallback_in_progress_{false};

#pragma once

#include "esphome/components/garuda_core/garuda_types.h"
#include <cstdint>

namespace garuda {
namespace eq {

#pragma pack(push, 1)
struct CurvePoint {
    percent_t fan_pct{0.0f};
    percent_t flap_pct{20.0f};
};

struct EqConfig {
    uint16_t schema_version{6};

    // Durable climate setpoints (AUT-02)
    kelvin_t desired_temp_k{295.15f}; // 22.0 °C
    percent_t desired_humidity_pct{60.0f};

    // Dynamic response ratios & aggression (AUT-02)
    percent_t fan_delta{10.0f};
    percent_t quiet_ratio{10.0f};
    percent_t perf_ratio{20.0f};
    uint8_t response_mode{1}; // 0=Manual (fan_delta), 1=Quiet (quiet_ratio), 2=Performance (perf_ratio)

    // Whole-device Operational Mode (CORE-001)
    // 0=OFF, 1=AUTO/TEMP, 2=AUTO/HUM, 3=MANUAL
    uint8_t operational_mode{1};
    bool fahrenheit_mode{true};
    bool tach_enabled{true};
    bool maintenance_lockout{false};
    bool fan_stall_latched{false}; // Persisted hardware safety latch (GLOB-26, FHT-01, FHT-02)

    // Fan Physical Limits
    percent_t fan_pwm_min{20.0f};
    percent_t fan_pwm_max{100.0f};
    float fan_slew_rate_pct_per_sec{10.0f};
    float fan_stall_rpm_threshold{100.0f};
    percent_t manual_fan_speed{0.0f};

    // Flap Actuator Configuration & Invariants
    bool flap_enabled{true};
    percent_t min_safe_flap_opening_pct{20.0f}; // Foundation curve opening (FFI-01, FLP-011)
    uint32_t flap_min_us{1000};
    uint32_t flap_max_us{2000};
    uint32_t flap_step_delay_ms{50};
    float flap_step_size_pct{1.0f};
    uint32_t flap_settle_time_ms{5000};
    bool flap_invert{false};
    percent_t manual_flap_target{0.0f};

    // Flap Proportional Monotonic Curve (CORE-026, FLP-02, FLP-05..13)
    bool flap_proportional_curve{true};
    uint8_t curve_point_count{3};
    CurvePoint curve_points[4]{
        {0.0f, 20.0f},
        {50.0f, 60.0f},
        {100.0f, 100.0f},
        {0.0f, 0.0f}
    };

    uint32_t crc32{0};
};
#pragma pack(pop)

    driver_.init(config);

    driver_.process();
}

    driver_.ingest_rpm(rpm);
}

void EqFanControl::ingest_current(float amps) {
    driver_.ingest_current(amps);
}

void EqFanControl::clear_fault() {
    driver_.clear_stall();
}

bool EqFanControl::command_speed(percent_t target_pct, bool e_stop_active, bool lockout_active) {
    if (e_stop_active) {
        ESP_LOGW(TAG, "Fan command rejected: Emergency Stop active.");
        driver_.emergency_stop();
        return false;
    }

    if (lockout_active && target_pct > 0.0f) {
        ESP_LOGW(TAG, "Fan command rejected: Maintenance Lockout active.");
        return false;
    }

    driver_.set_target(target_pct);
    return true;
}

FILE: E:\GARUDA\git\garuda-controls\sentinel\nodes\eq\components\eq_app\eq_flap_control.cpp, encoding=UTF-8
#include "eq_flap_control.h"
#include "esphome/core/log.h"
#include <algorithm>

static const char *const TAG = "garuda.eq_flap";

namespace garuda {
namespace eq {

EqFlapControl::EqFlapControl(ServoDriver &driver) : driver_(driver) {}

void EqFlapControl::init(const ServoConfig &config) {
    driver_.init(config);
}

void EqFlapControl::initial_seat() {
    driver_.force_seat_closed();
}

void EqFlapControl::process() {
    driver_.process();
}

percent_t EqFlapControl::calculate_curve_position(percent_t fan_speed_pct, const EqConfig &config) const {
    if (fan_speed_pct <= 0.0f) {
        return 0.0f;
    }

    if (!config.flap_proportional_curve) {
        return 100.0f; // Binary mode: full open on run (FLP-014)
    }

    if (config.curve_point_count < 2) {
        return std::clamp(fan_speed_pct, config.min_safe_flap_opening_pct, 100.0f);
    }

    // Piecewise linear interpolation across configured monotonic curve (FLP-02, FLP-07)
    if (fan_speed_pct <= config.curve_points[0].fan_pct) {
        return std::max(config.curve_points[0].flap_pct, config.min_safe_flap_opening_pct);
    }

    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < config.curve_point_count - 1; ++i) {
        float x0 = config.curve_points[i].fan_pct;
        float y0 = config.curve_points[i].flap_pct;
        float x1 = config.curve_points[i + 1].fan_pct;
        float y1 = config.curve_points[i + 1].flap_pct;

        if (fan_speed_pct >= x0 && fan_speed_pct <= x1) {
            float t = (x1 > x0) ? ((fan_speed_pct - x0) / (x1 - x0)) : 0.0f;
            float interpolated = y0 + t * (y1 - y0);
            return std::clamp(interpolated, config.min_safe_flap_opening_pct, 100.0f);
        }
    }

    return std::clamp(config.curve_points[config.curve_point_count - 1].flap_pct, config.min_safe_flap_opening_pct, 100.0f);
}

bool EqFlapControl::command_target(percent_t target_pct, bool e_stop_active, bool lockout_active) {
    if (e_stop_active) {
        ESP_LOGW(TAG, "Flap target rejected: Emergency Stop active.");
        driver_.emergency_detach();
        return false;
    }

    if (lockout_active) {
        ESP_LOGW(TAG, "Flap target rejected: Maintenance Lockout active.");
        return false;
    }

    driver_.set_target(target_pct);
    return true;
}

} // namespace eq
} // namespace garuda
