#include "eq_om_tables.h"

namespace garuda {
namespace eq {

OmResult EqOmTables::evaluate_fan_run(const EqSafetyContext &ctx) {
    // Declarative Fan OM Predicate Chain (Conjunctive AND)
    OmPredicate predicates[] = {
        // 1. E-Stop must be inactive
        { !ctx.emergency_stop_active, OmOutcome::PROHIBITED, ReasonCode::ESTOP_ACTIVE },

        // 2. Maintenance Lockout must be inactive
        { !ctx.maintenance_lockout_active, OmOutcome::PROHIBITED, ReasonCode::LOCKOUT_ACTIVE },

        // 3. Motor must not be in latched stall
        { !ctx.fan_stall_latched, OmOutcome::PROHIBITED, ReasonCode::MOTOR_STALL },

        // 4. Fundamental Safety Invariant (CORE-025, FFI-01):
        // Flap position must be >= min_safe_flap_opening OR flap capability disabled
        { (!ctx.flap_capability_enabled || ctx.current_flap_position >= ctx.min_safe_flap_opening),
          OmOutcome::PROHIBITED, ReasonCode::FLAP_INTERLOCK_BLOCKED }
    };

    return OperationalMatrix::evaluate(predicates, sizeof(predicates) / sizeof(predicates[0]));
}

OmResult EqOmTables::evaluate_flap_move(const EqSafetyContext &ctx, percent_t target_position) {
    bool is_closing_below_safe_min = (target_position < ctx.min_safe_flap_opening);

    // Declarative Flap OM Predicate Chain (Conjunctive AND)
    OmPredicate predicates[] = {
        // 1. E-Stop must be inactive
        { !ctx.emergency_stop_active, OmOutcome::PROHIBITED, ReasonCode::ESTOP_ACTIVE },

        // 2. Maintenance Lockout must be inactive
        { !ctx.maintenance_lockout_active, OmOutcome::PROHIBITED, ReasonCode::LOCKOUT_ACTIVE },

        // 3. Flap Closure Invariant (FFI-02):
        // Cannot close below safe threshold if fan is spinning or running
        { !(is_closing_below_safe_min && ctx.fan_is_spinning),
          OmOutcome::PROHIBITED, ReasonCode::FLAP_INTERLOCK_BLOCKED }
    };

    return OperationalMatrix::evaluate(predicates, sizeof(predicates) / sizeof(predicates[0]));
}

} // namespace eq
} // namespace garuda

FILE: E:\GARUDA\git\garuda-controls\sentinel\nodes\eq\components\eq_app\eq_om_tables.h, encoding=UTF-8
#pragma once

#include "esphome/components/garuda_core/operational_matrix.h"

namespace garuda {
namespace eq {

// Contextual system facts passed into the Fan and Flap matrices
struct EqSafetyContext {
    bool emergency_stop_active{false};
    bool maintenance_lockout_active{false};
    bool fan_stall_latched{false};
    bool fan_is_spinning{false};
    bool flap_capability_enabled{true};
    percent_t current_flap_position{0.0f};
    percent_t min_safe_flap_opening{20.0f}; // Harmonized with EqConfig (FFI-01)
};

class EqOmTables {
public:
    // Evaluates permission to start or run the ventilation fan (FFI-01)
    static OmResult evaluate_fan_run(const EqSafetyContext &ctx);

    // Evaluates permission to move or close the flap (FFI-02)
    static OmResult evaluate_flap_move(const EqSafetyContext &ctx, percent_t target_position);
};

} // namespace eq
} // namespace garuda

FILE: E:\GARUDA\git\garuda-controls\sentinel\nodes\eq\components\eq_app\eq_ui_controller.cpp, encoding=UTF-8
#include "eq_ui_controller.h"
#include "eq_app.h"
#include <cstdio>
#include <algorithm>

namespace garuda {
namespace eq {

EqUiController::EqUiController(EqApp &app) : app_(app) {
    display_sleep_timer_.reset();
    menu_timeout_timer_.reset();
}

void EqUiController::wake_display() {
    display_awake_ = true;
    display_sleep_timer_.reset();
}

void EqUiController::process_encoder_event(EncoderEvent event) {
    if (event == EncoderEvent::NONE) {
        if (display_awake_ && display_sleep_timer_.has_expired()) {
            display_awake_ = false;
            in_menu_mode_ = false;
        }
        if (in_menu_mode_ && menu_timeout_timer_.has_expired()) {
            in_menu_mode_ = false;
        }
        return;
    }

    // Any user interaction wakes the display
    if (!display_awake_) {
        wake_display();
        return;
    }
    wake_display();

    if (in_menu_mode_) {
        menu_timeout_timer_.reset();
    }

    switch (event) {
        case EncoderEvent::CLOCKWISE:
            handle_clockwise();
            break;
        case EncoderEvent::ANTICLOCKWISE:
            handle_anticlockwise();
            break;
        case EncoderEvent::CLICK:
            handle_click();
            break;
        case EncoderEvent::LONG_PRESS:
            in_menu_mode_ = !in_menu_mode_;
            menu_page_ = 0;
            menu_cursor_ = 0;
            break;
        default:
            break;
    }
}

void EqUiController::handle_clockwise() {
    if (in_menu_mode_) {
        uint8_t max_items = (menu_page_ == 0) ? 5 : 2;
        menu_cursor_ = (menu_cursor_ + 1) % max_items;
    } else {
        // Dashboard Direct Tuning based on active Mode
        const EqConfig &cfg = app_.get_config();
        if (cfg.operational_mode == 3) { // MANUAL: Tune Fan Speed
            app_.set_fan_run_speed(cfg.manual_fan_speed + 5.0f);
        } else if (cfg.operational_mode == 1) { // AUTO/TEMP: Tune Target Temp
            app_.update_desired_temperature(cfg.desired_temp_k + 0.5f);
        } else if (cfg.operational_mode == 2) { // AUTO/HUM: Tune Target Humidity
            app_.update_desired_humidity(cfg.desired_humidity_pct + 1.0f);
        }
    }
}

void EqUiController::handle_anticlockwise() {
    if (in_menu_mode_) {
        uint8_t max_items = (menu_page_ == 0) ? 5 : 2;
        menu_cursor_ = (menu_cursor_ + max_items - 1) % max_items;
    } else {
        const EqConfig &cfg = app_.get_config();
        if (cfg.operational_mode == 3) {
            app_.set_fan_run_speed(cfg.manual_fan_speed - 5.0f);
        } else if (cfg.operational_mode == 1) {
            app_.update_desired_temperature(cfg.desired_temp_k - 0.5f);
        } else if (cfg.operational_mode == 2) {
            app_.update_desired_humidity(cfg.desired_humidity_pct - 1.0f);
        }
    }
}

void EqUiController::handle_click() {
    if (!in_menu_mode_) {
        in_menu_mode_ = true;
        menu_page_ = 0;
        menu_cursor_ = 0;
        return;
    }

    const EqConfig &cfg = app_.get_config();

    if (menu_page_ == 0) {
        switch (menu_cursor_) {
            case 0: { // Cycle Mode: OFF (0) -> AUTO/T (1) -> AUTO/H (2) -> MANUAL (3)
                uint8_t next_mode = (cfg.operational_mode + 1) % 4;
                app_.set_operational_mode(next_mode);
                break;
            }
            case 1: { // Cycle Response Aggression: Manual (0) -> Quiet (1) -> Perf (2)
                uint8_t next_resp = (cfg.response_mode + 1) % 3;
                app_.set_response_mode(next_resp);
                break;
            }
            case 2: // Toggle Flap Manual Target: 0% <-> 100%
                app_.set_manual_flap_open(!app_.is_flap_open());
                break;
            case 3: // Enter Page 1 (Diagnostics & Faults)
                menu_page_ = 1;
                menu_cursor_ = 0;
                break;
            case 4: // Exit Menu
                in_menu_mode_ = false;
                break;
        }
    } else if (menu_page_ == 1) {
        switch (menu_cursor_) {
            case 0: // Clear Faults & Latches
                app_.clear_faults();
                break;
            case 1: // Back to Main Menu
                menu_page_ = 0;
                menu_cursor_ = 3;
                break;
        }
    }
}

void EqUiController::format_dashboard_line(uint8_t line_idx) {
    const ResourceEngine &res = app_.get_resources();
    const EqConfig &cfg = app_.get_config();

    auto to_disp_t = [&](float k) -> float {
        return cfg.fahrenheit_mode ? ((k - 273.15f) * 1.8f + 32.0f) : (k - 273.15f);
    };
    const char *unit_str = cfg.fahrenheit_mode ? "F" : "C";

    switch (line_idx) {
        case 0: { // Live Temperatures (Int / Ext)
            float t_in = res.get_canonical_value(ResourceId::RES_INTERNAL_TEMP);
            float t_out = res.get_canonical_value(ResourceId::RES_EXTERNAL_TEMP);
            snprintf(line_buffers_[0], sizeof(line_buffers_[0]), "IN:%.1f%s OUT:%.1f%s",
                     to_disp_t(t_in), unit_str, to_disp_t(t_out), unit_str);
            break;
        }
        case 1: { // Live Humidity (Int / Ext)
            float h_in = res.get_canonical_value(ResourceId::RES_INTERNAL_HUM);
            float h_out = res.get_canonical_value(ResourceId::RES_EXTERNAL_HUM);
            snprintf(line_buffers_[1], sizeof(line_buffers_[1]), "HUM: %.0f%% / %.0f%%", h_in, h_out);
            break;
        }
        case 2: { // Mode & Target Setpoint
            if (cfg.operational_mode == 1) {
                snprintf(line_buffers_[2], sizeof(line_buffers_[2]), "AUTO/T  SP:%.1f%s",
                         to_disp_t(cfg.desired_temp_k), unit_str);
            } else if (cfg.operational_mode == 2) {
                snprintf(line_buffers_[2], sizeof(line_buffers_[2]), "AUTO/H  SP:%.0f%%",
                         cfg.desired_humidity_pct);
            } else if (cfg.operational_mode == 3) {
                snprintf(line_buffers_[2], sizeof(line_buffers_[2]), "MANUAL  FAN:%.0f%%",
                         cfg.manual_fan_speed);
            } else {
                snprintf(line_buffers_[2], sizeof(line_buffers_[2]), "MODE: OFF");
            }
            break;
        }
        case 3: { // Actuator Telemetry & Badges
            const char *badge = "[OK]";
            if (app_.get_health_state() == SystemHealthState::MOTOR_STALL) badge = "[STALL]";
            else if (app_.get_health_state() == SystemHealthState::LOCKOUT) badge = "[LOCK]";
            else if (app_.get_health_state() == SystemHealthState::SENSOR_LOST) badge = "[SENS]";

            snprintf(line_buffers_[3], sizeof(line_buffers_[3]), "F:%.0f%% R:%.0f %s",
                     app_.get_fan_speed(), app_.get_fan_rpm(), badge);
            break;
        }
        default:
            line_buffers_[line_idx][0] = '\0';
            break;
    }
}

void EqUiController::format_menu_line(uint8_t line_idx) {
    const EqConfig &cfg = app_.get_config();

    if (menu_page_ == 0) {
        const char *items[5] = {
            "Mode",
            "Response",
            "Flap",
            "Diagnostics >",
            "< Exit Menu"
        };
        char val_buf[32];

        if (line_idx < 5) {
            char cursor = (menu_cursor_ == line_idx) ? '>' : ' ';
            if (line_idx == 0) {
                snprintf(line_buffers_[line_idx], sizeof(line_buffers_[line_idx]), "%c Mode: %s",
                         cursor, app_.get_mode_string());
            } else if (line_idx == 1) {
                const char *r_str = (cfg.response_mode == 1) ? "Quiet" : (cfg.response_mode == 2 ? "Perf" : "Manual");
                snprintf(line_buffers_[line_idx], sizeof(line_buffers_[line_idx]), "%c Resp: %s", cursor, r_str);
            } else if (line_idx == 2) {
                snprintf(line_buffers_[line_idx], sizeof(line_buffers_[line_idx]), "%c Flap: %s",
                         cursor, app_.is_flap_open() ? "OPEN" : "CLOSED");
            } else {
                snprintf(line_buffers_[line_idx], sizeof(line_buffers_[line_idx]), "%c %s", cursor, items[line_idx]);
            }
        }
    } else if (menu_page_ == 1) {
        if (line_idx == 0) {
            snprintf(line_buffers_[0], sizeof(line_buffers_[0]), "%c Clear Latches", (menu_cursor_ == 0) ? '>' : ' ');
        } else if (line_idx == 1) {
            snprintf(line_buffers_[1], sizeof(line_buffers_[1]), "%c < Back", (menu_cursor_ == 1) ? '>' : ' ');
        } else {
            line_buffers_[line_idx][0] = '\0';
        }
    }
}

const char *EqUiController::get_display_line(uint8_t line_idx) {
    if (line_idx >= 5) return "";

    if (!display_awake_) {
        return ""; // Display blanked in sleep
    }

    if (in_menu_mode_) {
        format_menu_line(line_idx);
    } else {
        format_dashboard_line(line_idx);
    }

    return line_buffers_[line_idx];
}

} // namespace eq
} // namespace garuda
