esphome:
  name: ${node_name}
  friendly_name: ${friendly_node_name}
  on_boot:
    # --- STAGE 1: HARDWARE & SAFETY INITIALIZATION ---
    - priority: 600
      then:
        - lambda: |-
            esp_reset_reason_t reason = esp_reset_reason();
            const char* reason_str = "Unknown";
            switch (reason) {
              case ESP_RST_POWERON:   reason_str = "Power On / Hard Reset"; break;
              case ESP_RST_SW:        reason_str = "Software Reboot"; break;
              case ESP_RST_PANIC:     reason_str = "System Panic (Crash)"; break;
              case ESP_RST_INT_WDT:   reason_str = "Interrupt Watchdog"; break;
              case ESP_RST_TASK_WDT:  reason_str = "Task Watchdog"; break;
              case ESP_RST_WDT:       reason_str = "Other Watchdog"; break;
              case ESP_RST_BROWNOUT:  reason_str = "Brownout (Voltage Drop)"; break;
              default:                reason_str = "Undefined"; break;
            }
            id(reset_reason_sensor).publish_state(reason_str);
            
            // Restore debounced setpoints from NVS to active RAM globals
            id(desired_temp_c) = id(nvs_desired_temp_c);
            id(desired_hum) = id(nvs_desired_hum);

            // Safety states
            id(target_fan_speed_percent) = id(current_fan_speed_percent);
            id(flap_is_open) = false;
            id(flap_manual_mode) = false;

        - servo.write: { id: flap_servo, level: -1.0 }

    # --- STAGE 2: WEB SERVER & ENTITY UI SYNCHRONIZATION ---
    - priority: -100.0  # Runs AFTER web server and networking are fully active
      then:
        - delay: 500ms
        - lambda: |-
            // 1. Publish Switches
            id(auto_mode_switch).publish_state(id(operational_mode) > 0);
            id(temp_control_switch).publish_state(id(operational_mode) == 1 || id(operational_mode) == 3);
            id(fahrenheit_mode_switch).publish_state(id(fahrenheit_mode));
            id(flap_manual_mode_switch).publish_state(id(flap_manual_mode));
            id(fan_lockout_switch).publish_state(id(fan_is_locked));
            id(tach_enabled_switch).publish_state(id(tach_enabled));

            // 2. Publish Sliders / Numbers
            id(fan_speed_slider).publish_state(id(target_fan_speed_percent));
            id(manual_flap_slider).publish_state(100);
            id(desired_temp_slider).publish_state(id(desired_temp_c));
            id(desired_hum_slider).publish_state(id(desired_hum));
            id(fan_delta_slider).publish_state(id(fan_delta));

            // 3. Publish Select Menus
            id(response_mode_select).publish_state(id(response_mode_select).current_option().c_str());
            id(internal_sensor_source).publish_state(id(internal_source_is_modbus) ? "Modbus Remote" : "Local Sensor 1");

            // 4. Publish Version & Diagnostic Text Sensors
            id(build_version).publish_state("build " + std::to_string(id(build_num)));
            id(build_version_string).publish_state("${build_version_string}");
            id(vent_custom_name_text).publish_state(id(vent_custom_name));

            ESP_LOGI("SYSTEM", "All entities synchronized to Web Server frontend.");

        - script.execute: display_timeout_script

esp32:
  board: esp32dev
  framework:

    sdkconfig_options:
      CONFIG_LWIP_MAX_SOCKETS: "20"
      CONFIG_HTTPD_MAX_OPEN_SOCKETS: "10"
      CONFIG_HTTPD_MAX_URI_LEN: "512"
      CONFIG_ESP32_BROWNOUT_DET: "y"
      CONFIG_ESP32_BROWNOUT_DET_LVL_SEL_0: "y" # Lowest trip point (2.43V) to survive brief motor starts
      CONFIG_ESP_TASK_WDT_TIMEOUT_S: "15" # Extends Task Watchdog so HTTP timeouts don't panic reboot

logger:

  initial_level: WARN # Boot default: 100% silent unless a real warning or error occurs
  logs:
    sensor: ERROR
    pulse_counter: ERROR
    i2c: ERROR
    esp-idf: WARN

prometheus:

# ==============================================================================
# RS485 & MODBUS CONFIGURATION
# ==============================================================================
uart:
  id: rs485_uart
  tx_pin: ${uart_tx_pin}
  rx_pin: ${uart_rx_pin}
  baud_rate: ${uart_baud_rate}
  stop_bits: 1
  rx_buffer_size: 1024

modbus: 
  id: modbus_hub
  uart_id: rs485_uart
  flow_control_pin: ${rs485_flow_pin}
  role: server

# ==============================================================================
# MODBUS REGISTER MAP: VENT SYSTEM SLAVE
# ==============================================================================
# Mapping Logic:
# - Group A (00-07): Live Environmental & Operational Telemetry
# - Group B (10-21): Primary Control Interfaces (Targets, Modes, Dynamic Ratios)
# - Group C (30-33): True External Statistics (Vent = Source of Truth)
# - Group D (40-44): System Health, Identity, and Diagnostics
# - Group E (50-57): Hub Environment & Climate Broadcast (AC State, Units)
# - Group F (60-67): Dynamic Cosmetic Name ("Cool Hat")
# - Group G (99):    System Level Commands
# ==============================================================================

# ==============================================================================
# MODBUS REGISTER MAP: VENT SYSTEM SLAVE
# ==============================================================================
modbus_server:
  - id: mb_server
    modbus_id: modbus_hub
    address: ${modbus_address}
    registers:

      # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
      # GROUP A: LIVE TELEMETRY (Read-Only for Hub)
      # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

      - address: 0
        value_type: S_WORD
        read_lambda: "return id(internal_temp_c).has_state() ? (int16_t)(id(internal_temp_c).state * 10) : 0;"

      - address: 1
        value_type: S_WORD
        read_lambda: "return id(internal_hum).has_state() ? (int16_t)(id(internal_hum).state * 10) : 0;"

      - address: 2
        value_type: S_WORD
        read_lambda: "return id(external_temp_c).has_state() ? (int16_t)(id(external_temp_c).state * 10) : 0;"

      - address: 3
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: "return id(external_hum).has_state() ? (uint16_t)(id(external_hum).state * 10) : 0;"

      - address: 4
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: "return (id(tach_enabled) && id(fan_rpm).has_state()) ? (uint16_t)id(fan_rpm).state : 0;"

      - address: 5
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: "return (uint16_t)id(current_fan_speed_percent);"

      - address: 6
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: "return id(flap_is_open) ? 1 : 0;"

      - address: 7 # System Health State (0:OK, 1:HubOffline, 2:SensorLost, 3:Stall, 4:Lockout)
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: "return (uint16_t)id(system_health_state);"

      # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
      # GROUP B: PRIMARY CONTROLS (Read/Write with Strict Validation)
      # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

      - address: 10 # Manual Speed Override (0-100%)
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: "return (uint16_t)id(target_fan_speed_percent);"
        write_lambda: |-
          if (x > 100) {
            ESP_LOGW("MODBUS", "Write rejected: Speed %d%% > 100%%", x);
            return false;
          }
          if (id(fan_is_locked) || id(fan_stall_latched)) {
            ESP_LOGW("MODBUS", "Write rejected: Fan is locked or stalled.");
            return false;
          }
          id(target_fan_speed_percent) = x;
          id(update_fan_speed).execute();
          return true;

      - address: 11 # Target Temperature Setpoint (10.0°C to 35.0°C / x10)
        value_type: S_WORD
        read_lambda: "return (int16_t)(id(desired_temp_c) * 10);"
        write_lambda: |-
          if (x < 100 || x > 350) {
            ESP_LOGW("MODBUS", "Write rejected: Target temp %d (x10) out of bounds [100..350]", x);
            return false;
          }
          id(desired_temp_c) = x / 10.0f;
          id(debounce_save_setpoints).execute();
          return true;

      - address: 12 # Target Humidity Setpoint (40% to 80%)
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: "return (uint16_t)id(desired_hum);"
        write_lambda: |-
          if (x < 40 || x > 80) {
            ESP_LOGW("MODBUS", "Write rejected: Target humidity %d%% out of bounds [40..80]", x);
            return false;
          }
          id(desired_hum) = x;
          id(debounce_save_setpoints).execute();
          return true;

      - address: 13 # Operational Mode (0..3)
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: "return (uint16_t)id(operational_mode);"
        write_lambda: |-
          if (x > 3) {
            ESP_LOGW("MODBUS", "Write rejected: Mode %d > 3", x);
            return false;
          }
          if (x == 0) {
            id(operational_mode) = 0;
            id(auto_mode_enabled) = false;
            id(auto_mode_switch).publish_state(false);
          } else if (x == 1) {
            id(operational_mode) = 1;
            id(auto_mode_enabled) = true;
            id(control_by_temp) = true;
            id(auto_mode_switch).publish_state(true);
            id(temp_control_switch).publish_state(true);
          } else if (x == 2) {
            id(operational_mode) = 2;
            id(auto_mode_enabled) = true;
            id(control_by_temp) = false;
            id(auto_mode_switch).publish_state(true);
            id(temp_control_switch).publish_state(false);
          } else if (x == 3) {
            id(operational_mode) = 3;
            id(auto_mode_enabled) = true;
            id(auto_mode_switch).publish_state(true);
          }
          return true;

      - address: 14 # Environmental Sensor Source (0=Local, 1=Remote/Hub)
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: "return id(internal_source_is_modbus) ? 1 : 0;"
        write_lambda: |-
          if (x > 1) return false;
          id(internal_sensor_source).make_call().set_option(x == 1 ? "Modbus Remote" : "Local Sensor 1").perform();
          return true;

      - address: 15 # Logic Aggression (0=Manual, 1=Quiet, 2=Performance)
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: |-
          auto m = id(response_mode_select).current_option();
          if (m == "Quiet") return 1;
          if (m == "Performance") return 2;
          return 0;
        write_lambda: |-
          if (x > 2) return false;
          auto call = id(response_mode_select).make_call();
          if (x == 1) call.set_option("Quiet");
          else if (x == 2) call.set_option("Performance");
          else call.set_option("Manual");
          call.perform();
          return true;

      - address: 16 # Fan Sensitivity Delta (5 to 50)
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: "return (uint16_t)id(fan_delta);"
        write_lambda: |-
          if (x < 5 || x > 50) {
            ESP_LOGW("MODBUS", "Write rejected: Fan delta %d out of bounds [5..50]", x);
            return false;
          }
          id(fan_delta) = x;
          return true;

      - address: 17 # Manual Flap Position (0 to 100%)
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: "return (uint16_t)id(manual_flap_slider).state;"
        write_lambda: |-
          if (x > 100) return false;
          id(manual_flap_slider).make_call().set_value(x).perform(); 
          return true;

      - address: 18 # Manual Flap Override Toggle (0=Auto, 1=Manual)
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: "return id(flap_manual_mode) ? 1 : 0;"
        write_lambda: |-
          if (x > 1) return false;
          if (x == 1) id(flap_manual_mode_switch).turn_on();
          else id(flap_manual_mode_switch).turn_off();
          return true;

      - address: 19 # Fan Maintenance Lockout (0=Unlocked, 1=Locked)
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: "return id(fan_is_locked) ? 1 : 0;"
        write_lambda: |-
          if (x > 1) return false;
          if (x == 1) id(fan_lockout_switch).turn_on();
          else id(fan_lockout_switch).turn_off();
          return true;

      - address: 20 # Dynamic Quiet Ratio (1 to 50)
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: "return (uint16_t)id(quiet_ratio);"
        write_lambda: |-
          if (x < 1 || x > 50) return false;
          id(quiet_ratio) = (int)x;
          return true;

      - address: 21 # Dynamic Performance Ratio (1 to 50)
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: "return (uint16_t)id(perf_ratio);"
        write_lambda: |-
          if (x < 1 || x > 50) return false;
          id(perf_ratio) = (int)x;
          return true;

      # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
      # GROUP C: TRUE EXTERNAL STATISTICS (Read-Only for Hub)
      # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

      - address: 30
        value_type: S_WORD
        read_lambda: "return (id(min_external_temp_c) <= -900.0) ? 0 : (int16_t)(id(min_external_temp_c) * 10);"

      - address: 31
        value_type: S_WORD
        read_lambda: "return (id(max_external_temp_c) <= -900.0) ? 0 : (int16_t)(id(max_external_temp_c) * 10);"

      - address: 32
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: "return (id(min_external_hum) <= -900.0) ? 0 : (uint16_t)(id(min_external_hum) * 10);"

      - address: 33
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: "return (id(max_external_hum) <= -900.0) ? 0 : (uint16_t)(id(max_external_hum) * 10);"

      # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
      # GROUP D: SYSTEM IDENTITY & HEALTH (Read-Only for Hub)
      # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

      - address: 40
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: "return ${build_version};"

      - address: 41
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: "return (uint16_t)id(i2c_int_error_count);"

      - address: 42
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: "return (uint16_t)id(i2c_ext_error_count);"

      - address: 43
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: "return (uint16_t)(millis() / 60000);"

      - address: 44
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: "return (uint16_t)esp_reset_reason();"

      # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
      # GROUP E: INTERNAL ENVIRONMENT REFLECTION & CLIMATE (Hub -> Vent)
      # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

      - address: 50
        value_type: S_WORD
        write_lambda: |-
          id(remote_int_temp_val) = (int16_t)x / 10.0f; 
          id(seconds_since_last_poll) = 0;
          return true;
        read_lambda: |-
          return std::isnan(id(remote_int_temp_val)) ? 0 : (int16_t)(id(remote_int_temp_val) * 10);

      - address: 51
        value_type: U_WORD
        write_lambda: |-
          id(remote_int_hum_val) = x / 10.0f; 
          return true;
        read_lambda: |-
          return std::isnan(id(remote_int_hum_val)) ? 0 : (uint16_t)(id(remote_int_hum_val) * 10);

      - address: 52
        value_type: S_WORD
        write_lambda: |-
          id(min_internal_temp_c) = (int16_t)x / 10.0f; 
          return true;
        read_lambda: |-
          return (id(min_internal_temp_c) <= -900.0) ? 0 : (int16_t)(id(min_internal_temp_c) * 10);

      - address: 53
        value_type: S_WORD
        write_lambda: |-
          id(max_internal_temp_c) = (int16_t)x / 10.0f; 
          return true;
        read_lambda: |-
          return (id(max_internal_temp_c) <= -900.0) ? 0 : (int16_t)(id(max_internal_temp_c) * 10);

      - address: 54
        value_type: U_WORD
        write_lambda: |-
          id(min_internal_hum) = x / 10.0f; 
          return true;
        read_lambda: |-
          return (id(min_internal_hum) <= -900.0) ? 0 : (uint16_t)(id(min_internal_hum) * 10);

      - address: 55
        value_type: U_WORD
        write_lambda: |-
          id(max_internal_hum) = x / 10.0f; 
          return true;
        read_lambda: |-
          return (id(max_internal_hum) <= -900.0) ? 0 : (uint16_t)(id(max_internal_hum) * 10);

      - address: 56
        value_type: U_WORD
        write_lambda: |-
          if (x > 5) return false;
          id(ac_state_broadcast) = (uint16_t)x;
          id(seconds_since_last_poll) = 0;
          return true;
        read_lambda: "return id(ac_state_broadcast);"

      - address: 57
        value_type: U_WORD
        write_lambda: |-
          if (x > 1) return false;
          id(fahrenheit_mode) = (x == 1);
          id(fahrenheit_mode_switch).publish_state(x == 1);
          if (!id(oled_display).is_failed()) id(oled_display).update();
          return true;
        read_lambda: "return id(fahrenheit_mode) ? 1 : 0;"

      # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
      # GROUP F: DYNAMIC DEVICE NAMING (EQ-06: Modbus Registers 60-67)
      # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

      - address: 60
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: |-
          const auto &n = id(vent_custom_name);
          uint8_t c0 = (n.size() > 0) ? (uint8_t)n[0] : 0;
          uint8_t c1 = (n.size() > 1) ? (uint8_t)n[1] : 0;
          return (uint16_t)(c0 | (c1 << 8));
        write_lambda: |-
          if (id(vent_custom_name).size() < 2) id(vent_custom_name).resize(2, ' ');
          id(vent_custom_name)[0] = (char)(x & 0xFF);
          id(vent_custom_name)[1] = (char)((x >> 8) & 0xFF);
          return true;

      - address: 61
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: |-
          const auto &n = id(vent_custom_name);
          uint8_t c0 = (n.size() > 2) ? (uint8_t)n[2] : 0;
          uint8_t c1 = (n.size() > 3) ? (uint8_t)n[3] : 0;
          return (uint16_t)(c0 | (c1 << 8));
        write_lambda: |-
          if (id(vent_custom_name).size() < 4) id(vent_custom_name).resize(4, ' ');
          id(vent_custom_name)[2] = (char)(x & 0xFF);
          id(vent_custom_name)[3] = (char)((x >> 8) & 0xFF);
          return true;

      - address: 62
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: |-
          const auto &n = id(vent_custom_name);
          uint8_t c0 = (n.size() > 4) ? (uint8_t)n[4] : 0;
          uint8_t c1 = (n.size() > 5) ? (uint8_t)n[5] : 0;
          return (uint16_t)(c0 | (c1 << 8));
        write_lambda: |-
          if (id(vent_custom_name).size() < 6) id(vent_custom_name).resize(6, ' ');
          id(vent_custom_name)[4] = (char)(x & 0xFF);
          id(vent_custom_name)[5] = (char)((x >> 8) & 0xFF);
          return true;

      - address: 63
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: |-
          const auto &n = id(vent_custom_name);
          uint8_t c0 = (n.size() > 6) ? (uint8_t)n[6] : 0;
          uint8_t c1 = (n.size() > 7) ? (uint8_t)n[7] : 0;
          return (uint16_t)(c0 | (c1 << 8));
        write_lambda: |-
          if (id(vent_custom_name).size() < 8) id(vent_custom_name).resize(8, ' ');
          id(vent_custom_name)[6] = (char)(x & 0xFF);
          id(vent_custom_name)[7] = (char)((x >> 8) & 0xFF);
          return true;

      - address: 64
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: |-
          const auto &n = id(vent_custom_name);
          uint8_t c0 = (n.size() > 8) ? (uint8_t)n[8] : 0;
          uint8_t c1 = (n.size() > 9) ? (uint8_t)n[9] : 0;
          return (uint16_t)(c0 | (c1 << 8));
        write_lambda: |-
          if (id(vent_custom_name).size() < 10) id(vent_custom_name).resize(10, ' ');
          id(vent_custom_name)[8] = (char)(x & 0xFF);
          id(vent_custom_name)[9] = (char)((x >> 8) & 0xFF);
          return true;

      - address: 65
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: |-
          const auto &n = id(vent_custom_name);
          uint8_t c0 = (n.size() > 10) ? (uint8_t)n[10] : 0;
          uint8_t c1 = (n.size() > 11) ? (uint8_t)n[11] : 0;
          return (uint16_t)(c0 | (c1 << 8));
        write_lambda: |-
          if (id(vent_custom_name).size() < 12) id(vent_custom_name).resize(12, ' ');
          id(vent_custom_name)[10] = (char)(x & 0xFF);
          id(vent_custom_name)[11] = (char)((x >> 8) & 0xFF);
          return true;

      - address: 66
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: |-
          const auto &n = id(vent_custom_name);
          uint8_t c0 = (n.size() > 12) ? (uint8_t)n[12] : 0;
          uint8_t c1 = (n.size() > 13) ? (uint8_t)n[13] : 0;
          return (uint16_t)(c0 | (c1 << 8));
        write_lambda: |-
          if (id(vent_custom_name).size() < 14) id(vent_custom_name).resize(14, ' ');
          id(vent_custom_name)[12] = (char)(x & 0xFF);
          id(vent_custom_name)[13] = (char)((x >> 8) & 0xFF);
          return true;

      - address: 67
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: |-
          const auto &n = id(vent_custom_name);
          uint8_t c0 = (n.size() > 14) ? (uint8_t)n[14] : 0;
          uint8_t c1 = (n.size() > 15) ? (uint8_t)n[15] : 0;
          return (uint16_t)(c0 | (c1 << 8));
        write_lambda: |-
          if (id(vent_custom_name).size() < 16) id(vent_custom_name).resize(16, ' ');
          id(vent_custom_name)[14] = (char)(x & 0xFF);
          id(vent_custom_name)[15] = (char)((x >> 8) & 0xFF);
          return true;

      # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
      # GROUP G: SYSTEM COMMANDS
      # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

      - address: 99
        value_type: U_WORD
        read_lambda: "return 0;"
        write_lambda: |-
          if (x == 1) id(reboot_btn).press(); 
          return true;

# ==============================================================================
# HARDWARE BUSES & UI ASSETS
# ==============================================================================
i2c:
  - id: bus_internal
    sda: 21
    scl: 22
    scan: true
    frequency: ${i2c_frequency}
    timeout: ${i2c_timeout_ms}
  - id: bus_external
    sda: 18
    scl: 19
    scan: true
    frequency: ${i2c_frequency}
    timeout: ${i2c_timeout_ms}

font:
  - file: "gfonts://Roboto"
    id: big_font
    size: 16
  - file: "gfonts://Roboto"
    id: small_font
    size: 12

# ==============================================================================
# GLOBALS (SYSTEM CONTEXT & DIAGNOSTICS)
# ==============================================================================
globals:
  # --- 1. Industrial Control & Health ---
  # Bit 0(1):Sensors, Bit 1(2):Auto-Logic, Bit 2(4):Actuators, Bit 3(8):UI, Bit 4(16):Watchdog
  - id: vent_logic_mask
    type: int
    restore_value: yes
    initial_value: "31"

  # System Health State: 0:OK, 1:HubOffline, 2:SensorLost, 3:FanStall(Latched), 4:Lockout
  - id: system_health_state
    type: int
    initial_value: "0"

  - id: system_debug_level
    type: int
    initial_value: "0" # 0: Silent, 1: Status, 2: Debug

  - id: master_tick_counter
    type: int
    initial_value: "0"

  - id: seconds_since_last_poll
    type: uint32_t
    initial_value: "0"

  # Hardware Bus Error Counters
  - id: i2c_int_error_count
    type: uint32_t
    initial_value: "0"
  - id: i2c_ext_error_count
    type: uint32_t
    initial_value: "0"
  - id: i2c_int_consecutive_errors
    type: int
    initial_value: "0"
  - id: i2c_ext_consecutive_errors
    type: int
    initial_value: "0"

  # Fan Stall Watchdog (Latched Protection)
  - id: fan_stall_latched
    type: bool
    initial_value: "false"
  - id: fan_stall_timer_s
    type: int
    initial_value: "0"

  # Network Failover & Exponential Backoff
  - id: http_consecutive_failures
    type: int
    initial_value: "0"

  # --- 2. System Identity & Display ---
  - id: build_num
    type: int
    initial_value: "${build_version}"
  - id: fahrenheit_mode
    type: bool
    restore_value: yes
    initial_value: "true"
  - id: display_is_on
    type: bool
    initial_value: "true"
  - id: show_min_stats
    type: bool
    initial_value: "true"
  
  # EQ-06: Dynamic NVS Cosmetic Name
  - id: vent_custom_name
    type: std::string
    restore_value: yes
    initial_value: '"Vent"'

  # --- 3. Fan & Logic State ---
  - id: current_fan_speed_percent
    type: int
    initial_value: "0"
  - id: target_fan_speed_percent
    type: int
    initial_value: "0"
  
  # Maintenance Hardware Lockout (High Safety - Stored in NVS)
  - id: fan_is_locked
    type: bool
    restore_value: yes
    initial_value: "false"

  # EQ-01: Operational Mode: 0=Manual, 1=Temp-Auto, 2=Hum-Auto, 3=Compliance
  - id: operational_mode
    type: int
    restore_value: yes
    initial_value: "1"

  # Redundant flags kept in RAM to eliminate flash wear
  - id: auto_mode_enabled
    type: bool
    restore_value: no
    initial_value: "true"
  - id: control_by_temp
    type: bool
    restore_value: no
    initial_value: "true"

  # Setpoints: Active working variables in RAM
  - id: desired_temp_c
    type: float
    restore_value: no
    initial_value: "${default_target_temp_c}"
  - id: desired_hum
    type: int
    restore_value: no
    initial_value: "${default_target_humidity}"

  # Setpoints: Debounced Flash NVS backing storage
  - id: nvs_desired_temp_c
    type: float
    restore_value: yes
    initial_value: "${default_target_temp_c}"
  - id: nvs_desired_hum
    type: int
    restore_value: yes
    initial_value: "${default_target_humidity}"

  - id: fan_delta
    type: int
    restore_value: yes
    initial_value: "${default_fan_delta}"

  # EQ-03: Dynamic Response Ratio Registers
  - id: quiet_ratio
    type: int
    restore_value: yes
    initial_value: "${default_quiet_ratio}"
  - id: perf_ratio
    type: int
    restore_value: yes
    initial_value: "${default_perf_ratio}"

  # EQ-01: AC Broadcast State (0=Off, 1=PreCool, 2=Cool, 3=Heat, 4=Idle, 5=Fan)
  - id: ac_state_broadcast
    type: uint16_t
    initial_value: "0"

  - id: ramp_interval
    type: int
    initial_value: "${fan_ramp_interval_ms}"
  - id: internal_source_is_modbus
    type: bool
    restore_value: yes
    initial_value: "true"
  - id: remote_int_temp_val
    type: float
    initial_value: "NAN"
  - id: remote_int_hum_val
    type: float
    initial_value: "NAN"
  - id: reason_code
    type: int
    initial_value: "0"

  # --- 4. Flap & Servo Transit ---
  - id: flap_is_open
    type: bool
    initial_value: "false"
  - id: flap_manual_mode
    type: bool
    restore_value: no # Failsafe: Reset to Auto on reboot
    initial_value: "false"
  - id: flap_current_level
    type: float
    initial_value: "-1.0"
  - id: flap_target_level
    type: float
    initial_value: "-1.0"
  - id: flap_step_delay
    type: int
    initial_value: "${servo_step_delay_ms}"
  - id: flap_step_size
    type: float
    initial_value: "${servo_step_size}"

  # Anti-Freeze Background Routine Containers
  - id: last_anti_freeze_time_s
    type: uint32_t
    initial_value: "0"
  - id: anti_freeze_in_progress
    type: bool
    initial_value: "false"

  # Dead-Man Sensor Loss Watchdog Containers
  - id: sensor_loss_counter_s
    type: uint32_t
    initial_value: "0"
  - id: sensor_fault_active
    type: bool
    initial_value: "false"

  # --- 5. UI Menu State ---
  - id: in_menu_mode
    type: bool
    initial_value: "false"
  - id: menu_selection
    type: int
    initial_value: "0"
  - id: menu_page
    type: int
    initial_value: "0"
  - id: encoder_control_mode
    type: int
    initial_value: "0"

  # --- 6. Environmental Min/Max (RAM Only) ---
  - id: min_internal_temp_c
    type: float
    initial_value: "-999.0"
  - id: max_internal_temp_c
    type: float
    initial_value: "-999.0"
  - id: min_external_temp_c
    type: float
    initial_value: "-999.0"
  - id: max_external_temp_c
    type: float
    initial_value: "-999.0"
  - id: min_internal_hum
    type: float
    initial_value: "-999.0"
  - id: max_internal_hum
    type: float
    initial_value: "-999.0"
  - id: min_external_hum
    type: float
    initial_value: "-999.0"
  - id: max_external_hum
    type: float
    initial_value: "-999.0"

  - id: tach_enabled
    type: bool
    restore_value: yes
    initial_value: "true"

# ==============================================================================
# MASTER HEARTBEAT (Consolidated Timing)
# ==============================================================================
interval:
  - interval: 1s
    then:
      - output.turn_on: onboard_led
      - delay: 50ms
      - output.turn_off: onboard_led

  - interval: 1s
    then:
      - lambda: |-
          // 1. Tick Management (0-9)
          id(master_tick_counter)++;
          if (id(master_tick_counter) >= 10) id(master_tick_counter) = 0;
          int tick = id(master_tick_counter);
          uint8_t mask = id(vent_logic_mask);

          id(seconds_since_last_poll)++;

          // 2. HARDWARE FAN STALL / JAM DETECTION WATCHDOG
          if (id(tach_enabled) && id(current_fan_speed_percent) >= 30) {
            float rpm = id(fan_rpm).has_state() ? id(fan_rpm).state : 0.0f;
            if (rpm < 100.0f) {
              id(fan_stall_timer_s)++;
              if (id(fan_stall_timer_s) >= 5) {
                if (!id(fan_stall_latched)) {
                  id(fan_stall_latched) = true;
                  ESP_LOGE("HARDWARE", "CRITICAL: Fan motor stall or tachometer loss detected! Power cut to protect motor.");
                  id(target_fan_speed_percent) = 0;
                  id(update_fan_speed).execute();
                }
              }
            } else {
              id(fan_stall_timer_s) = 0;
            }
          } else {
            id(fan_stall_timer_s) = 0;
          }

          // 3. DEAD-MAN SENSOR LOSS WATCHDOG
          bool has_valid_sensor = false;
          if (id(internal_source_is_modbus)) {
            has_valid_sensor = !std::isnan(id(remote_int_temp_val));
          } else {
            has_valid_sensor = !std::isnan(id(local_1_temp).state);
          }

          if (!has_valid_sensor) {
            id(sensor_loss_counter_s)++;
            if (id(sensor_loss_counter_s) >= ${sensor_deadman_timeout_s}) {
              if (!id(sensor_fault_active)) {
                id(sensor_fault_active) = true;
                ESP_LOGE("WATCHDOG", "DEAD-MAN: Sensors lost >%ds! Halting actuators.", ${sensor_deadman_timeout_s});
                id(vent_auto_logic_engine).execute();
              }
            }
          } else {
            id(sensor_loss_counter_s) = 0;
            if (id(sensor_fault_active)) {
              id(sensor_fault_active) = false;
              if (id(system_debug_level) >= 1) ESP_LOGI("WATCHDOG", "DEAD-MAN: Valid sensor data restored.");
            }
          }

          // 4. CONSOLIDATED SYSTEM HEALTH EVALUATION
          if (id(fan_stall_latched)) {
            id(system_health_state) = 3; // Error: Motor Stall
          } else if (id(sensor_fault_active)) {
            id(system_health_state) = 2; // Error: Sensor Lost
          } else if (id(fan_is_locked)) {
            id(system_health_state) = 4; // Lockout Engaged
          } else if (id(seconds_since_last_poll) > ${compliance_failsafe_timeout_s}) {
            id(system_health_state) = 1; // Warning: Hub Offline
          } else {
            id(system_health_state) = 0; // OK
          }

          // 5. COMPLIANCE MODE HEARTBEAT FAILSAFE
          if (id(operational_mode) == 3 && id(seconds_since_last_poll) > ${compliance_failsafe_timeout_s}) {
            id(operational_mode) = 1;
            id(auto_mode_enabled) = true;
            id(control_by_temp) = true;
            id(auto_mode_switch).publish_state(true);
            id(temp_control_switch).publish_state(true);
            ESP_LOGW("WATCHDOG", "Hub ping lost in Compliance Mode! Reverting to Standalone Temp-Auto.");
          }

          // 6. SMART COMM WATCHDOG (Bit 4 / Value 16)
          if ((mask & 16) && id(seconds_since_last_poll) > ${sensor_failsafe_timeout_s}) {
            if (id(internal_source_is_modbus)) {
              id(internal_source_is_modbus) = false;
              id(internal_sensor_source).publish_state("Local Sensor 1 (FAILSAFE)");
              ESP_LOGE("WATCHDOG", "Hub Lost! Reverting to Local Sensors for safety.");
            }
          }

          // 7. STAGGERED I2C SENSOR POLLING (Bit 0 / Value 1)
          if (mask & 1) {
            if (tick == 0 && !id(local_sht30_int).is_failed()) id(local_sht30_int).update(); 
            if (tick == 1) {
              if (id(local_sht30_int).is_failed() || std::isnan(id(local_1_temp).state)) {
                id(i2c_int_error_count)++;
                id(i2c_int_consecutive_errors)++;
              } else {
                id(i2c_int_consecutive_errors) = 0;
              }
              id(internal_temp_c).update();
              id(internal_hum).update();
            }
            
            if (tick == 3 && !id(local_sht30_ext).is_failed()) id(local_sht30_ext).update(); 
            if (tick == 4) {
              if (id(local_sht30_ext).is_failed() || std::isnan(id(local_2_temp).state)) {
                id(i2c_ext_error_count)++;
                id(i2c_ext_consecutive_errors)++;
              } else {
                id(i2c_ext_consecutive_errors) = 0;
              }
              id(external_temp_c).update();
              id(external_hum).update();
            }
          }

          // 8. AUTO-LOGIC ENGINE (Bit 1 / Value 2)
          if ((mask & 2) && (tick == 2 || tick == 7)) {
            id(vent_auto_logic_engine).execute();
          }

          // 9. ANTI-FREEZE INSPECTION (Tick 8)
          if (tick == 8 && id(external_temp_c).has_state() && !id(anti_freeze_in_progress)) {
            float ext_t = id(external_temp_c).state;
            uint32_t now_s = millis() / 1000;
            if (ext_t > -50.0f && ext_t < ${anti_freeze_threshold_c}) {
              if ((now_s - id(last_anti_freeze_time_s)) >= ${anti_freeze_cycle_interval_s}) {
                id(last_anti_freeze_time_s) = now_s;
                id(execute_anti_freeze_cycle).execute();
              }
            }
          }

          // 10. HTTP FAILOVER PUSH WITH EXPONENTIAL BACKOFF
          if (id(seconds_since_last_poll) > ${http_fallback_trigger_s}) {
            int backoff_interval = 10;
            if (id(http_consecutive_failures) >= 5) backoff_interval = 300; // 5m backoff
            else if (id(http_consecutive_failures) >= 2) backoff_interval = 30; // 30s backoff

            if (id(master_tick_counter) % (backoff_interval >= 10 ? 10 : backoff_interval) == 6) {
              id(push_telemetry_to_hub).execute();
            }
          }

          // 11. UI REFRESH (Bit 3 / Value 8)
          if (mask & 8) {
            if (tick % 3 == 0) id(show_min_stats) = !id(show_min_stats);
            if (tick % 2 == 0 && !id(oled_display).is_failed()) id(oled_display).update();
          }
# ==============================================================================
# UI SELECT MENUS (Configuration States)
# ==============================================================================
select:
  # --- 1. RESPONSE AGGRESSION MODE ---
  - platform: template
    name: "Response Mode"
    id: response_mode_select
    optimistic: true
    restore_value: true
    initial_option: "Quiet"
    options: ["Manual", "Quiet", "Performance"]
    icon: "mdi:speedometer"
    on_value:
      then:
        - lambda: |-
            if (id(system_debug_level) >= 1) {
              ESP_LOGI("UI", "Response Mode changed to: %s", x.c_str());
            }

  # --- 2. INTERNAL SENSOR SOURCE SELECTION ---
  - platform: template
    name: "Internal Sensor Source"
    id: internal_sensor_source
    optimistic: true
    restore_value: true
    initial_option: "Modbus Remote"
    options: ["Local Sensor 1", "Modbus Remote"]
    set_action:
      - lambda: |-
          id(internal_source_is_modbus) = (x == "Modbus Remote");
          if (id(system_debug_level) >= 1) {
            ESP_LOGI("UI", "Sensor Source changed to: %s", x.c_str());
          }
          id(internal_temp_c).update();
          id(internal_hum).update();

  # --- 3. DYNAMIC ON-THE-FLY LOGGING SELECTOR ---
  - platform: template
    name: "System Logging Level"
    id: system_logging_select
    optimistic: true
    initial_option: "Silent (Warn/Error)"
    options: ["Silent (Warn/Error)", "Status (Info)", "Debug (All)"]
    icon: "mdi:bug"
    set_action:
      - lambda: |-
          if (x == "Silent (Warn/Error)") {
            id(system_debug_level) = 0;
          } else if (x == "Status (Info)") {
            id(system_debug_level) = 1;
          } else {
            id(system_debug_level) = 2;
          }
      - if:
          condition:
            lambda: 'return x == "Silent (Warn/Error)";'
          then:
            - logger.set_level: WARN
          else:
            - if:
                condition:
                  lambda: 'return x == "Status (Info)";'
                then:
                  - logger.set_level: INFO
                else:
                  - logger.set_level: DEBUG
      - lambda: |-
          ESP_LOGW("LOGGER", "Logging verbosity set on-the-fly to: %s", x.c_str());

# ==============================================================================
# HARDWARE OUTPUT DRIVERS (PWM & SERVO)
# ==============================================================================
output:
  # Flap Servo PWM (50Hz standard for servos)
  - platform: ledc
    id: flap_servo_pwm
    pin: ${flap_servo_pin}
    frequency: 50Hz
    channel: 0

  # Main Fan PWM (High frequency to eliminate audible whine)
  - platform: ledc
    pin: ${fan_pwm_pin}
    id: fan_pwm
    frequency: ${fan_pwm_frequency}
    channel: 8

  # Onboard Heartbeat Status LED
  - platform: gpio
    pin:
      number: ${status_led_pin}
      inverted: false      # Set to true if your board's LED is active-low
    id: onboard_led

servo:
  - id: flap_servo
    output: flap_servo_pwm
    auto_detach_time: ${servo_auto_detach_time} # Cuts PWM holding power 1.5s after transit finishes
    min_level: 12%     
    max_level: 3%    
    idle_level: 7.5%

# ==============================================================================
# SCRIPTS (AUTOMATION WORKERS)
# ==============================================================================
script:
  # --- 1. FAN RAMPING & FLAP COORDINATION ---
  - id: update_fan_speed
    mode: restart
    then:
      - lambda: |-
          // Latched Hardware Stall Interlock
          if (id(fan_stall_latched)) {
            ESP_LOGE("SAFETY", "Fan output disabled: Motor stall fault is LATCHED. Clear fault to resume.");
            id(target_fan_speed_percent) = 0;
          }

          // Maintenance Lockout Interlock
          if (id(fan_is_locked) && id(target_fan_speed_percent) > 0) {
            ESP_LOGW("SAFETY", "Fan spin-up blocked by Maintenance Lockout!");
            id(target_fan_speed_percent) = 0;
          }

          if (!(id(vent_logic_mask) & 4)) {
            if (id(system_debug_level) >= 1) ESP_LOGW("ACTUATOR", "Fan update ignored: Actuators Masked.");
            return;
          }
          if (id(system_debug_level) >= 1) {
            ESP_LOGI("ACTUATOR", "Fan Ramping: %d%% -> %d%%", id(current_fan_speed_percent), id(target_fan_speed_percent));
          }

      # --- OPENING LOGIC ---
      - if:
          condition:
            lambda: "return id(target_fan_speed_percent) > 0 && !id(flap_is_open) && !id(fan_stall_latched);"
          then:
            - globals.set: { id: flap_manual_mode, value: "false" }
            - switch.template.publish: { id: flap_manual_mode_switch, state: OFF }
            - globals.set: { id: flap_is_open, value: "true" }
            - globals.set: { id: flap_target_level, value: "1.0" }
            - script.execute: move_flap_smoothly
            - script.wait: move_flap_smoothly
            - delay: 1s 

      # --- FAN RAMPING ENGINE ---
      - if:
          condition:
            lambda: "return id(current_fan_speed_percent) == 0 && id(target_fan_speed_percent) > 0 && id(target_fan_speed_percent) < 20;"
          then:
            - output.set_level: { id: fan_pwm, level: 0.20 }
            - delay: 2s
            - output.set_level: { id: fan_pwm, level: !lambda "return id(target_fan_speed_percent) / 100.0;" }
            - globals.set: { id: current_fan_speed_percent, value: !lambda "return id(target_fan_speed_percent);" }
          else:
            - while:
                condition:
                  lambda: "return id(current_fan_speed_percent) != id(target_fan_speed_percent);"
                then:
                  - if:
                      condition: { lambda: "return id(current_fan_speed_percent) < id(target_fan_speed_percent);" }
                      then:
                        - globals.set: { id: current_fan_speed_percent, value: !lambda "return id(current_fan_speed_percent) + 1;" }
                      else:
                        - globals.set: { id: current_fan_speed_percent, value: !lambda "return id(current_fan_speed_percent) - 1;" }
                  - output.set_level: { id: fan_pwm, level: !lambda "return id(current_fan_speed_percent) / 100.0;" }
                  - delay: !lambda "return id(ramp_interval);"

      # --- TACHOMETER-VERIFIED SAFE CLOSING LOGIC ---
      - if:
          condition:
            lambda: |-
              return id(target_fan_speed_percent) == 0 && id(flap_is_open) && !id(flap_manual_mode);
          then:
            - if:
                condition:
                  lambda: "return id(tach_enabled);"
                then:
                  - if:
                      condition:
                        lambda: "return id(system_debug_level) >= 1;"
                      then:
                        - logger.log: "Fan commanded to 0%. Monitoring tachometer for safe spin-down..."
                  - wait_until:
                      condition:
                        lambda: |-
                          if (id(target_fan_speed_percent) > 0) return true;
                          float current_rpm = id(fan_rpm).has_state() ? id(fan_rpm).state : 0.0f;
                          return (current_rpm < ${rpm_close_threshold});
                      timeout: ${rpm_spin_down_timeout_s}s
                else:
                  - if:
                      condition:
                        lambda: "return id(system_debug_level) >= 1;"
                      then:
                        - logger.log: "Fan commanded to 0% (Tach Disabled). Waiting fixed spin-down delay..."
                  - delay: 3s
            - if:
                condition:
                  lambda: "return id(target_fan_speed_percent) == 0 && id(flap_is_open) && !id(flap_manual_mode);"
                then:
                  - lambda: |-
                      float final_rpm = (id(tach_enabled) && id(fan_rpm).has_state()) ? id(fan_rpm).state : 0.0f;
                      if (id(system_debug_level) >= 1) {
                        if (id(tach_enabled)) {
                          ESP_LOGI("ACTUATOR", "Tachometer safe (RPM: %.0f < %d). Sealing flap.", final_rpm, ${rpm_close_threshold});
                        } else {
                          ESP_LOGI("ACTUATOR", "Spin-down delay elapsed. Sealing flap.");
                        }
                      }
                  - globals.set: { id: flap_is_open, value: "false" }
                  - globals.set: { id: flap_target_level, value: "-1.0" }
                  - script.execute: move_flap_smoothly
                else:
                  - if:
                      condition:
                        lambda: "return id(system_debug_level) >= 1;"
                      then:
                        - logger.log: "Fan re-engaged during spin-down period. Aborting flap closure."

  # --- 2. SERVO TRANSIT LOGIC WITH HARD ITERATION GUARD ---
  - id: move_flap_smoothly
    mode: restart
    then:
      - if:
          condition:
            lambda: "return (id(vent_logic_mask) & 4);"
          then:
            - lambda: |-
                // NaN Verification Guard
                if (std::isnan(id(flap_current_level)) || std::isnan(id(flap_target_level))) {
                  ESP_LOGE("SERVO", "Transit aborted: Target or Current level is NaN!");
                  id(flap_target_level) = -1.0;
                  id(flap_current_level) = -1.0;
                  id(flap_servo)->write(-1.0);
                  return;
                }

                int step_count = 0;
                const int MAX_STEPS = 65; // Hard ceiling to prevent infinite loops

                while (abs(id(flap_current_level) - id(flap_target_level)) > 0.005f && step_count < MAX_STEPS) {
                  step_count++;
                  if (id(flap_current_level) < id(flap_target_level)) {
                    id(flap_current_level) += id(flap_step_size);
                    if (id(flap_current_level) > id(flap_target_level)) id(flap_current_level) = id(flap_target_level);
                  } else {
                    id(flap_current_level) -= id(flap_step_size);
                    if (id(flap_current_level) < id(flap_target_level)) id(flap_current_level) = id(flap_target_level);
                  }
                  id(flap_servo)->write(id(flap_current_level));
                  delay(id(flap_step_delay));
                }

                if (step_count >= MAX_STEPS) {
                  ESP_LOGW("SERVO", "Step limit reached. Forcing servo to final target: %.2f", id(flap_target_level));
                  id(flap_current_level) = id(flap_target_level);
                  id(flap_servo)->write(id(flap_target_level));
                }
          else:
            - lambda: 'if(id(system_debug_level) >= 1) ESP_LOGW("ACTUATOR", "Flap move ignored: Masked.");'

  # --- 3. UI & OLED TIMEOUTS ---
  - id: menu_timeout_script
    mode: restart
    then:
      - delay: 10s
      - globals.set: { id: in_menu_mode, value: "false" }
      - if:
          condition:
            lambda: 'return !id(oled_display).is_failed();'
          then:
            - component.update: oled_display

  - id: display_timeout_script
    mode: restart
    then:
      - if:
          condition:
            lambda: 'return (id(vent_logic_mask) & 8) && !id(oled_display).is_failed();'
          then:
            - delay: 30s
            - lambda: |-
                id(oled_display).turn_off();
                id(display_is_on) = false;

  # --- 4. DATA PROCESSING ---
  - id: process_internal_temp
    mode: restart 
    then:
      - if:
          condition: { lambda: "return (id(vent_logic_mask) & 1);" }
          then:
            - lambda: |-
                float val = id(internal_temp_c).state;
                if (std::isnan(val)) return;
                
                if (id(min_internal_temp_c) < -900.0) {
                  id(min_internal_temp_c) = val; id(max_internal_temp_c) = val;
                } else {
                  if (val < id(min_internal_temp_c)) id(min_internal_temp_c) = val;
                  if (val > id(max_internal_temp_c)) id(max_internal_temp_c) = val;
                }
                id(min_internal_temp_sensor).publish_state(id(min_internal_temp_c));
                id(max_internal_temp_sensor).publish_state(id(max_internal_temp_c));
                id(internal_temp_sensor).publish_state(val); 

  - id: process_internal_hum
    mode: restart
    then:
      - if:
          condition: { lambda: "return (id(vent_logic_mask) & 1);" }
          then:
            - lambda: |-
                float val = id(internal_hum).state;
                if (std::isnan(val)) return;
                if (id(min_internal_hum) == -999.0 || val < id(min_internal_hum)) id(min_internal_hum) = val;
                if (val > id(max_internal_hum)) id(max_internal_hum) = val;
                id(min_internal_hum_sensor).publish_state(id(min_internal_hum));
                id(max_internal_hum_sensor).publish_state(id(max_internal_hum));
                id(internal_hum_sensor).publish_state(val); 

  # --- 5. AUTO LOGIC ENGINE ---
  - id: vent_auto_logic_engine
    mode: restart
    then:
      - if:
          condition: { lambda: "return id(operational_mode) > 0;" }
          then:
            - lambda: |-
                // Latched Fan Stall Fault Interlock
                if (id(fan_stall_latched)) {
                  id(reason_code) = 98; // Motor Stall Error
                  if (id(target_fan_speed_percent) != 0) {
                    id(target_fan_speed_percent) = 0;
                    id(update_fan_speed).execute();
                  }
                  return;
                }

                // Dead-Man Sensor Loss Protection
                if (id(sensor_fault_active)) {
                  id(reason_code) = 99; // Sensor Fault
                  if (id(target_fan_speed_percent) != 0) {
                    id(target_fan_speed_percent) = 0;
                    id(update_fan_speed).execute();
                  }
                  return;
                }

                // Maintenance Lockout Protection
                if (id(fan_is_locked)) {
                  if (id(target_fan_speed_percent) != 0) {
                    id(target_fan_speed_percent) = 0;
                    id(update_fan_speed).execute();
                  }
                  return;
                }

                int next_speed = 0;
                int delta = id(fan_delta);
                
                auto mode_str = id(response_mode_select).current_option();
                if (mode_str == "Quiet") delta = id(quiet_ratio);
                else if (mode_str == "Performance") delta = id(perf_ratio);

                // ========================================================
                // MODE 3: COMPLIANCE MODE WITH SMART THERMAL PURGE
                // ========================================================
                if (id(operational_mode) == 3) {
                  uint16_t ac_state = id(ac_state_broadcast);
                  float i_t = id(internal_temp_c).state;
                  float e_t = id(external_temp_c).state;
                  float target = id(desired_temp_c);

                  if (i_t <= (target + 0.5f) || i_t <= e_t || ac_state == 0 || ac_state == 4) {
                    next_speed = 0;
                    id(reason_code) = 4; // Ideal Point Met -> Sealed
                  } else if (ac_state == 1 || ac_state == 2) {
                    next_speed = (int)(id(perf_ratio) * (i_t - target)); // Active Thermal Purge
                    id(reason_code) = 0;
                  } else {
                    next_speed = 0;
                    id(reason_code) = 0;
                  }
                }

                // ========================================================
                // MODE 1: TEMPERATURE AUTO
                // ========================================================
                else if (id(operational_mode) == 1) {
                  if (id(internal_temp_c).has_state() && id(external_temp_c).has_state()) {
                    float i_t = id(internal_temp_c).state;
                    float e_t = id(external_temp_c).state;
                    float target = id(desired_temp_c);

                    if (i_t <= e_t) {
                      next_speed = 0;
                      id(reason_code) = 1; // Outside Hot
                    } else {
                      float diff = i_t - target;
                      if (diff > 0.5f) {
                        next_speed = (int)(diff * (float)delta);
                        id(reason_code) = 0; 
                      } else {
                        next_speed = 0;
                        id(reason_code) = 0;
                      }
                    }
                  }
                }

                // ========================================================
                // MODE 2: HUMIDITY AUTO
                // ========================================================
                else if (id(operational_mode) == 2) {
                  if (id(internal_hum).has_state() && id(external_hum).has_state()) {
                    float i_h = id(internal_hum).state;
                    float e_h = id(external_hum).state;
                    float target = (float)id(desired_hum);

                    if (i_h <= e_h) {
                      next_speed = 0;
                      id(reason_code) = 2; // Outside Humid
                    } else {
                      float diff = i_h - target;
                      if (diff > 3.0f) {
                        next_speed = (int)(diff * (float)delta);
                        id(reason_code) = 0;
                      } else {
                        next_speed = 0;
                        id(reason_code) = 0;
                      }
                    }
                  }
                }

                next_speed = clamp(next_speed, 0, 100);

                if (id(system_debug_level) >= 2 && next_speed != id(target_fan_speed_percent)) {
                  ESP_LOGD("LOGIC", "OpMode:%d | Speed:%d%% -> %d%% | Reason:%d", 
                           id(operational_mode), id(target_fan_speed_percent), next_speed, id(reason_code));
                }

                if (next_speed != id(target_fan_speed_percent)) {
                  id(target_fan_speed_percent) = next_speed;
                  id(update_fan_speed).execute();
                }

  - id: reset_comm_watchdog
    then:
      - lambda: |-
          id(seconds_since_last_poll) = 0;
          if (id(internal_sensor_source).current_option() == "Modbus Remote") {
             id(internal_source_is_modbus) = true;
          }

  # --- 6. ANTI-FREEZE SERVO EXERCISE ROUTINE ---
  - id: execute_anti_freeze_cycle
    mode: restart
    then:
      - if:
          condition:
            lambda: |-
              return (id(vent_logic_mask) & 4) && 
                     !id(flap_is_open) && 
                     !id(flap_manual_mode) && 
                     (id(current_fan_speed_percent) == 0);
          then:
            - globals.set: { id: anti_freeze_in_progress, value: "true" }
            - if:
                condition:
                  lambda: "return id(system_debug_level) >= 1;"
                then:
                  - logger.log: "SAFETY: Anti-freeze cycle triggered. Flexing flap..."
            - globals.set: { id: flap_target_level, value: "1.0" }
            - script.execute: move_flap_smoothly
            - script.wait: move_flap_smoothly
            - delay: !lambda "return ${anti_freeze_flex_dwell_ms};"
            - globals.set: { id: flap_target_level, value: "-1.0" }
            - script.execute: move_flap_smoothly
            - script.wait: move_flap_smoothly
            - globals.set: { id: anti_freeze_in_progress, value: "false" }
            - if:
                condition:
                  lambda: "return id(system_debug_level) >= 1;"
                then:
                  - logger.log: "SAFETY: Anti-freeze cycle complete. Flap re-sealed."

  # --- 7. DEBOUNCED NVS FLASH WRITE ENGINE ---
  - id: debounce_save_setpoints
    mode: restart
    then:
      - delay: !lambda "return ${setpoint_debounce_s} * 1000;"
      - lambda: |-
          id(nvs_desired_temp_c) = id(desired_temp_c);
          id(nvs_desired_hum) = id(desired_hum);
          if (id(system_debug_level) >= 1) {
            ESP_LOGI("NVS", "Debounced setpoints committed to flash: %.1f°C, %d%%", id(desired_temp_c), id(desired_hum));
          }

  # --- 8. HTTP TELEMETRY PUSH WITH EXPONENTIAL BACKOFF ---
  - id: push_telemetry_to_hub
    mode: restart
    then:
      - if:
          condition:
            lambda: "return id(vent_wifi).is_connected();"
          then:
            - http_request.post:
                url: !lambda |-
                  auto clean_nan = [](float val) -> float { return std::isnan(val) ? 0.0f : val; };
                  char buf[256];
                  snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%s,%.1f,%.1f,%.1f,%.1f,%d,%d,%d,%d,%d,%d,%d,%d,%d",
                    "${mailbox_shared_key}",
                    clean_nan(id(internal_temp_c).state), clean_nan(id(internal_hum).state),
                    clean_nan(id(external_temp_c).state), clean_nan(id(external_hum).state),
                    (int)((id(tach_enabled) && id(fan_rpm).has_state()) ? id(fan_rpm).state : 0),
                    id(current_fan_speed_percent),
                    id(flap_is_open) ? (id(flap_manual_mode) ? (int)id(manual_flap_slider).state : 100) : 0,
                    id(reason_code),
                    id(operational_mode),
                    id(fan_delta),
                    (int)(millis() / 60000),
                    (int)esp_reset_reason(),
                    id(build_num)
                  );
                  return "http://${hub_ip}/text/vent_mailbox/set?value=" + std::string(buf);
                request_headers:
                  Authorization: !secret hub_auth_header
                on_response:
                  then:
                    - lambda: |-
                        id(http_consecutive_failures) = 0;
                        if (id(system_debug_level) >= 2) ESP_LOGD("HTTP_MAILBOX", "Telemetry pushed to Hub successfully.");
                on_error:
                  then:
                    - lambda: |-
                        id(http_consecutive_failures)++;
                        if (id(system_debug_level) >= 1) {
                          ESP_LOGW("HTTP_MAILBOX", "Hub unreachable at %s (Failures: %d). Backing off.", "${hub_ip}", id(http_consecutive_failures));
                        }

# ==============================================================================
# UI CONTROL SLIDERS (Number Entities)
# ==============================================================================
number:
  # --- 1. MANUAL FAN SPEED ---
  - platform: template
    name: "Manual Fan Speed"
    id: fan_speed_slider
    min_value: 0
    max_value: 100
    step: 1
    unit_of_measurement: "%"
    lambda: "return id(target_fan_speed_percent);"
    set_action:
      - lambda: |-
          if (id(fan_is_locked)) {
            ESP_LOGW("SAFETY", "Manual Fan Speed rejected: Fan is LOCKED.");
            return;
          }
          if (!(id(vent_logic_mask) & 4)) {
            ESP_LOGW("UI", "Fan Slider Ignored: Actuators Masked.");
            return;
          }
          if (id(system_debug_level) >= 1) ESP_LOGI("UI", "Manual Fan Speed set to: %.0f%%", x);
          
          id(operational_mode) = 0;
          id(auto_mode_switch).publish_state(false);
          id(target_fan_speed_percent) = (int)x;
          id(update_fan_speed).execute();

  # --- 2. MANUAL FLAP POSITION ---
  - platform: template
    name: "Manual Flap Position"
    id: manual_flap_slider
    min_value: 0
    max_value: 100
    step: 1
    unit_of_measurement: "%"
    mode: SLIDER
    optimistic: true
    initial_value: 100
    set_action:
      then:
        - lambda: |-
            if (!(id(vent_logic_mask) & 4)) {
              ESP_LOGW("UI", "Flap Slider Ignored: Actuators Masked.");
              return;
            }
            if (id(flap_manual_mode) && id(target_fan_speed_percent) == 0) {
              if (id(system_debug_level) >= 1) ESP_LOGI("UI", "Manual Flap Position set to: %.0f%%", x);
              id(flap_target_level) = (x / 50.0f) - 1.0f;
              id(move_flap_smoothly).execute();
            } else {
              ESP_LOGW("UI", "Flap move blocked: Ensure Fan is 0 and Flap Manual Mode is ON.");
            }

  # --- 3. TARGET TEMPERATURE ---
  - platform: template
    name: "Desired Temperature"
    id: desired_temp_slider
    min_value: 10
    max_value: 35
    step: 0.5
    unit_of_measurement: "°C"
    device_class: temperature
    mode: SLIDER
    lambda: "return id(desired_temp_c);"
    set_action:
      - lambda: |-
          if (!(id(vent_logic_mask) & 2)) {
            ESP_LOGW("UI", "Temp Slider Ignored: Logic Engine Masked.");
            return;
          }
          id(desired_temp_c) = x;
          id(debounce_save_setpoints).execute();
          if (id(system_debug_level) >= 1) ESP_LOGI("UI", "Target Temp updated: %.1f°C", x);
          
          char buf[20];
          sprintf(buf, "%.1f °F", (x * 1.8f + 32.0f));
          id(f_temp_display).publish_state(buf);

  # --- 4. TARGET HUMIDITY ---
  - platform: template
    name: "Desired Humidity"
    id: desired_hum_slider
    min_value: 40
    max_value: 80
    step: 1
    unit_of_measurement: "%"
    lambda: "return id(desired_hum);"
    set_action:
      - lambda: |-
          if (!(id(vent_logic_mask) & 2)) return;
          id(desired_hum) = (int)x;
          id(debounce_save_setpoints).execute();
          if (id(system_debug_level) >= 1) ESP_LOGI("UI", "Target Hum updated: %d%%", (int)x);

  # --- 5. SENSITIVITY DELTA ---
  - platform: template
    name: "Fan Response Rate"
    id: fan_delta_slider
    min_value: 5
    max_value: 50
    step: 1
    unit_of_measurement: "% / Δ"
    lambda: "return id(fan_delta);"
    set_action:
      - lambda: |-
          if (!(id(vent_logic_mask) & 2)) return;
          id(fan_delta) = (int)x;
          id(response_mode_select).make_call().set_option("Manual").perform();
          if (id(system_debug_level) >= 1) ESP_LOGI("UI", "Sensitivity set to: %d%% per degree", (int)x);

# ==============================================================================
# UI TOGGLES (Switch Entities)
# ==============================================================================
switch:
  # --- 1. DISPLAY UNIT TOGGLE (Bit 3 / Value 8) ---
  - platform: template
    name: "Display in Fahrenheit"
    id: fahrenheit_mode_switch
    restore_mode: RESTORE_DEFAULT_ON
    lambda: "return id(fahrenheit_mode);"
    turn_on_action:
      - lambda: |-
          id(fahrenheit_mode) = true;
          if (id(system_debug_level) >= 1) ESP_LOGI("UI", "Units set to Fahrenheit");
    turn_off_action:
      - lambda: |-
          id(fahrenheit_mode) = false;
          if (id(system_debug_level) >= 1) ESP_LOGI("UI", "Units set to Celsius");

  # --- 2. AUTO LOGIC TOGGLE (Bit 1 / Value 2) ---
  - platform: template
    name: "Auto Mode"
    id: auto_mode_switch
    restore_mode: RESTORE_DEFAULT_ON
    lambda: "return id(operational_mode) > 0;"
    turn_on_action:
      - lambda: |-
          if (!(id(vent_logic_mask) & 2)) {
            ESP_LOGW("LOGIC", "Auto Mode block: Logic Engine Masked.");
            return;
          }
          if (id(operational_mode) == 0) {
            id(operational_mode) = id(control_by_temp) ? 1 : 2;
          }
          id(auto_mode_enabled) = true;
          id(encoder_control_mode) = id(control_by_temp) ? 1 : 2;
          if (id(system_debug_level) >= 1) ESP_LOGI("LOGIC", "Auto Mode ENABLED (OpMode %d)", id(operational_mode));
    turn_off_action:
      - lambda: |-
          id(operational_mode) = 0;
          id(auto_mode_enabled) = false;
          id(encoder_control_mode) = 0;
          if (id(system_debug_level) >= 1) ESP_LOGI("LOGIC", "Auto Mode DISABLED (Manual)");

  # --- 3. SENSOR CONTROL TOGGLE (Bit 1 / Value 2) ---
  - platform: template
    name: "Temperature Control"
    id: temp_control_switch
    restore_mode: RESTORE_DEFAULT_ON
    lambda: "return id(control_by_temp);"
    turn_on_action:
      - lambda: |-
          id(control_by_temp) = true;
          if (id(operational_mode) == 2) id(operational_mode) = 1;
          if (id(operational_mode) > 0) id(encoder_control_mode) = 1;
          if (id(system_debug_level) >= 1) ESP_LOGI("LOGIC", "Control Target: TEMPERATURE");
    turn_off_action:
      - lambda: |-
          id(control_by_temp) = false;
          if (id(operational_mode) == 1) id(operational_mode) = 2;
          if (id(operational_mode) > 0) id(encoder_control_mode) = 2;
          if (id(system_debug_level) >= 1) ESP_LOGI("LOGIC", "Control Target: HUMIDITY");

  # --- 4. MANUAL FLAP TOGGLE (Bit 2 / Value 4) ---
  - platform: template
    name: "Manual Flap Mode"
    id: flap_manual_mode_switch
    icon: "mdi:hand-back-right"
    lambda: "return id(flap_manual_mode);"
    turn_on_action:
      - lambda: |-
          if (!(id(vent_logic_mask) & 4)) return;
          if (id(current_fan_speed_percent) > 0) {
            ESP_LOGE("HARDWARE", "Safety Block: Cannot move flap while fan is spinning!");
            id(flap_manual_mode_switch).publish_state(false);
            return;
          }
          id(flap_manual_mode) = true;
          id(flap_target_level) = (id(manual_flap_slider).state / 50.0f) - 1.0f;
          id(move_flap_smoothly).execute();
          if (id(system_debug_level) >= 1) ESP_LOGI("HARDWARE", "Flap: MANUAL MODE ON");
    turn_off_action:
      - lambda: |-
          id(flap_manual_mode) = false;
          id(update_fan_speed).execute();
          if (id(system_debug_level) >= 1) ESP_LOGI("HARDWARE", "Flap: MANUAL MODE OFF (Auto)");

  # --- 5. FAN MAINTENANCE LOCKOUT SWITCH ---
  - platform: template
    name: "Fan Maintenance Lockout"
    id: fan_lockout_switch
    icon: "mdi:fan-lock"
    restore_mode: RESTORE_DEFAULT_OFF
    lambda: "return id(fan_is_locked);"
    turn_on_action:
      - lambda: |-
          id(fan_is_locked) = true;
          id(target_fan_speed_percent) = 0;
          id(update_fan_speed).execute();
          ESP_LOGW("SAFETY", "Fan Lockout ENGAGED. Fan forced to 0%% and blocked.");
    turn_off_action:
      - lambda: |-
          id(fan_is_locked) = false;
          ESP_LOGI("SAFETY", "Fan Lockout RELEASED. Normal operation restored.");
          if (id(operational_mode) > 0) {
            id(vent_auto_logic_engine).execute();
          }

# --- 6. FAN TACHOMETER MONITORING TOGGLE ---
  - platform: template
    name: "Fan Tachometer Monitoring"
    id: tach_enabled_switch
    icon: "mdi:gauge"
    restore_mode: RESTORE_DEFAULT_ON
    lambda: "return id(tach_enabled);"
    turn_on_action:
      - lambda: |-
          id(tach_enabled) = true;
          ESP_LOGI("SAFETY", "Tachometer feedback & stall watchdog ENABLED.");
    turn_off_action:
      - lambda: |-
          id(tach_enabled) = false;
          // Clear any active stall latch so disabling tach instantly unblocks fan
          id(fan_stall_latched) = false;
          id(fan_stall_timer_s) = 0;
          ESP_LOGW("SAFETY", "Tachometer feedback DISABLED. Stall watchdog bypassed & faults cleared.");

# ==============================================================================
# SYSTEM BUTTONS
# ==============================================================================
button:
  - platform: restart
    name: "Reboot Device"
    id: reboot_btn
    on_press:
      - lambda: 'ESP_LOGW("SYSTEM", "Vent System Rebooting...");'

  - platform: template
    name: "Reset Min-Max Stats"
    id: reset_stats_btn
    icon: "mdi:restore"
    on_press:
      - lambda: |-
          id(min_internal_temp_c) = -999.0; id(max_internal_temp_c) = -999.0;
          id(min_external_temp_c) = -999.0; id(max_external_temp_c) = -999.0;
          id(min_internal_hum) = -999.0;    id(max_internal_hum) = -999.0;
          id(min_external_hum) = -999.0;    id(max_external_hum) = -999.0;
          
          id(min_internal_temp_sensor).publish_state(NAN);
          id(max_internal_temp_sensor).publish_state(NAN);
          id(min_external_temp_sensor).publish_state(NAN);
          id(max_external_temp_sensor).publish_state(NAN);
          id(min_internal_hum_sensor).publish_state(NAN);
          id(max_internal_hum_sensor).publish_state(NAN);
          id(min_external_hum_sensor).publish_state(NAN);
          id(max_external_hum_sensor).publish_state(NAN);
          
          if (id(system_debug_level) >= 1) ESP_LOGI("DATA", "Statistics cleared successfully.");

  - platform: template
    name: "Clear Latched Faults"
    id: clear_faults_btn
    icon: "mdi:alert-remove"
    on_press:
      - lambda: |-
          id(fan_stall_latched) = false;
          id(fan_stall_timer_s) = 0;
          ESP_LOGI("SAFETY", "Latched faults cleared by user. Normal operation unblocked.");
          id(vent_auto_logic_engine).execute();

# ==============================================================================
# PHYSICAL UI INPUTS (Binary Sensors)
# ==============================================================================
binary_sensor:
  - platform: gpio
    pin:
      number: 23
      mode: INPUT_PULLUP
    id: encoder_button
    name: "Encoder Button"
    on_press:
      then:
        - lambda: |-
            if (!(id(vent_logic_mask) & 8)) {
              if (id(system_debug_level) >= 1) ESP_LOGW("UI", "Encoder click ignored: UI Masked.");
              return;
            }

            if (id(system_debug_level) >= 2) ESP_LOGD("UI", "Encoder Button Pressed");

            if (!id(display_is_on)) {
              id(display_is_on) = true;
              if (!id(oled_display).is_failed()) {
                id(oled_display).turn_on();
                id(display_timeout_script).execute();
                id(oled_display).update();
              }
              if (id(system_debug_level) >= 2) ESP_LOGD("UI", "Display Woken by Encoder");
              return; 
            }

            id(display_timeout_script).execute();

            if (id(in_menu_mode)) {
              id(menu_timeout_script).execute();
              
              if (id(menu_page) == 0) {
                switch(id(menu_selection)) {
                  case 0: // Cycle Modes: Manual (0) -> Temp (1) -> Hum (2) -> Compliance (3)
                    {
                      int next_m = (id(operational_mode) + 1) % 4;
                      id(operational_mode) = next_m;
                      if (next_m == 0) {
                        id(auto_mode_switch).publish_state(false);
                      } else if (next_m == 1) {
                        id(auto_mode_switch).publish_state(true);
                        id(control_by_temp) = true;
                        id(temp_control_switch).publish_state(true);
                      } else if (next_m == 2) {
                        id(auto_mode_switch).publish_state(true);
                        id(control_by_temp) = false;
                        id(temp_control_switch).publish_state(false);
                      } else if (next_m == 3) {
                        id(auto_mode_switch).publish_state(true);
                      }
                    }
                    break;
                  case 1: // Toggle Temp/Hum Target
                    if (id(operational_mode) == 0) id(auto_mode_switch).turn_on();
                    id(temp_control_switch).toggle();
                    break;
                  case 2: // Cycle Response Aggression
                    {
                      auto cur = id(response_mode_select).current_option();
                      auto call = id(response_mode_select).make_call();
                      if (cur == "Manual") call.set_option("Quiet");
                      else if (cur == "Quiet") call.set_option("Performance");
                      else call.set_option("Manual");
                      call.perform();
                    }
                    break;
                  case 3: // Enter Sensor Sub-Menu
                    id(menu_page) = 1; 
                    id(menu_selection) = 0; 
                    break;
                  case 4: // Toggle Units
                    id(fahrenheit_mode_switch).toggle();
                    break;
                }
              } else if (id(menu_page) == 1) {
                if (id(menu_selection) == 0) {
                  auto call = id(internal_sensor_source).make_call();
                  if (id(internal_source_is_modbus)) call.set_option("Local Sensor 1");
                  else call.set_option("Modbus Remote");
                  call.perform();
                } else if (id(menu_selection) == 1) {
                  id(menu_page) = 0; 
                  id(menu_selection) = 3; 
                }
              }
            } else {
              id(in_menu_mode) = true;
              id(menu_timeout_script).execute();
              if (id(system_debug_level) >= 2) ESP_LOGD("UI", "Entered Menu Mode");
            }

            if (!id(oled_display).is_failed()) id(oled_display).update();

  # --- 7. PHYSICAL UI INPUT (Rotary Encoder) ---
  - platform: rotary_encoder
    name: "Control Knob"
    id: control_knob
    pin_a: 14
    pin_b: 13
    on_clockwise:
      then:
        - lambda: |-
            if (!(id(vent_logic_mask) & 8)) return;
            if (!id(display_is_on)) {
              id(display_is_on) = true;
              if (!id(oled_display).is_failed()) id(oled_display).turn_on();
              id(display_timeout_script).execute(); return;
            }
            id(display_timeout_script).execute();
            if (id(in_menu_mode)) {
              int max_items = (id(menu_page) == 1) ? 2 : 5;
              id(menu_selection) = (id(menu_selection) + 1) % max_items;
            } else {
              if (id(encoder_control_mode) == 0) {
                if (id(fan_is_locked)) {
                  ESP_LOGW("UI", "Encoder fan change blocked: Fan is LOCKED.");
                } else {
                  id(target_fan_speed_percent) = clamp(id(target_fan_speed_percent) + 5, 0, 100);
                  id(update_fan_speed).execute();
                }
              } else if (id(encoder_control_mode) == 1) {
                float step = id(fahrenheit_mode) ? (1.0f/1.8f) : 0.5f;
                id(desired_temp_c) = clamp(id(desired_temp_c) + step, 15.0f, 30.0f);
                id(debounce_save_setpoints).execute();
              } else if (id(encoder_control_mode) == 2) {
                id(desired_hum) = clamp(id(desired_hum) + 1, 40, 80);
                id(debounce_save_setpoints).execute();
              }
            }
            if (!id(oled_display).is_failed()) id(oled_display).update();

    on_anticlockwise:
      then:
        - lambda: |-
            if (!(id(vent_logic_mask) & 8)) return;
            if (!id(display_is_on)) {
              id(display_is_on) = true;
              if (!id(oled_display).is_failed()) id(oled_display).turn_on();
              id(display_timeout_script).execute(); return;
            }
            id(display_timeout_script).execute();
            if (id(in_menu_mode)) {
              int max_items = (id(menu_page) == 1) ? 2 : 5;
              id(menu_selection) = (id(menu_selection) + max_items - 1) % max_items;
            } else {
              if (id(encoder_control_mode) == 0) {
                if (id(fan_is_locked)) {
                  ESP_LOGW("UI", "Encoder fan change blocked: Fan is LOCKED.");
                } else {
                  id(target_fan_speed_percent) = clamp(id(target_fan_speed_percent) - 5, 0, 100);
                  id(update_fan_speed).execute();
                }
              } else if (id(encoder_control_mode) == 1) {
                float step = id(fahrenheit_mode) ? (1.0f/1.8f) : 0.5f;
                id(desired_temp_c) = clamp(id(desired_temp_c) - step, 15.0f, 30.0f);
                id(debounce_save_setpoints).execute();
              } else if (id(encoder_control_mode) == 2) {
                id(desired_hum) = clamp(id(desired_hum) - 1, 40, 80);
                id(debounce_save_setpoints).execute();
              }
            }
            if (!id(oled_display).is_failed()) id(oled_display).update();
