## Stage 5: Fan Actuator Domain & Tachometer Feedback

*Goal: Bring up the physical ventilation fan with PWM ramp profiling, startup boost, relay sequencing, and tachometer feedback.*

### 1. Architectural Scope & Deployment State

* Implements the isolated `FanDriver` (`FAN-01..05`).
- Enforces startup boost to prevent cold motor stalls, and orderly power-down dissipation delays.

### 2. Files Created

* `global/drivers/fan_driver.h` & `.cpp` (LEDC PWM, relay GPIO, tachometer PCNT ISR).
- `nodes/eq/controllers/eq_fan_control.h` & `.cpp` (Fan ramp profiler, tachless mode handling).

### 3. Concrete Parameters & Configuration

* **Hardware Pins:** PWM Pin 25 (1000 Hz), Tach Pin 27 (PCNT input, 13 µs filter), Power Relay GPIO.
- **Ramp Rate:** $5.0\%/\text{sec}$ slew rate.
- **Startup Sequence:** Flap check $\to$ Relay ON $\to 500\text{ ms}$ settle $\to 2.0\text{s}$ @ $20\%$ PWM boost $\to$ Target PWM.
- **Shutdown Sequence:** Slew to $0\% \to$ confirm motor stop $\to 10\text{s}$ thermal dissipation delay $\to$ Relay OFF.
- **Stall Threshold:** RPM $<100$ when commanded speed $\ge 30\%$ for 5 consecutive seconds.

### 4. Deployable Health Check

* Command fan from $0\%$ to $10\%$.
- Verify relay clicks first, pauses 500 ms, boosts to 20% for 2 seconds, and settles down to 10%. Tachometer correctly reads motor RPM. Commanding $0\%$ keeps relay engaged for 10 seconds after PWM stops.

---

## Stage 6: Fan ↔ Flap Safety Invariant & Local Operational Matrix

*Goal: Implement the core safety rule: the fan must NEVER run against a closed flap, and the flap must NEVER close against a spinning fan.*

### 1. Architectural Scope & Deployment State

* Replaces fragmented safety `if/else` checks across ESPHome scripts with compiled, declarative Boolean equations (`GLOB-20`, `GLOB-36`, `FFI-01..04`).

### 2. Files Created

* `global/core/operational_matrix.h` (Zero-allocation conjunctive AND template evaluator).
- `nodes/eq/eq_om_tables.h` & `.cpp` (Compiled matrices for Fan and Flap domains).

### 3. Concrete Rules & Invariants Formally Enforced

* **Fundamental Invariant:** $\mathbf{FAN\_RUNNING} \implies \mathbf{FLAP\_SATISFIES\_REQUIRED\_MINIMUM\_OPENING}$
- **Fan Startup Gate:** Fan Driver is locked in `OM_PROHIBITED` until Flap Driver confirms position $\ge \text{Point 1 Opening}$ (default $20\%$).
- **Flap Closure Gate:** Flap Driver close command ($<20\%$) is held in pending state until Fan Driver confirms physical stop ($0\text{ RPM}$ or relay open).
- **Manual Close Takeover:** Operator commanding Flap $0\%$ while fan is active automatically commands Fan to $0\%$, waits for full stop, then closes the flap.

### 4. Deployable Health Check

* With fan running at $50\%$, manually force the flap slider to $0\%$.
- Verify the system immediately ramps fan to $0$, waits for tachometer to report 0 RPM, and only then seals the flap shut.

---

## Stage 7: Autonomous Climate Engine & Mode Management

*Goal: Implement the whole-device operating modes and proportional environmental equilibrium calculations.*

### 1. Architectural Scope & Deployment State

* Replaces legacy `vent_auto_logic_engine` script with native C++ Kelvin floating-point control loops (`AUT-01..04`, `MCA-01..03`).
- Operates 100% autonomously without any external network dependency.

### 2. Files Created

* `nodes/eq/controllers/eq_climate_engine.h` & `.cpp` (Equilibrium gating, response ratios, floor profiles).
- `nodes/eq/controllers/eq_mode_manager.h` & `.cpp` (Modes: `OFF`, `AUTO/TEMP`, `AUTO/HUM`, `MANUAL`).

### 3. Concrete Parameters & Configuration

* **Modes:** `OFF (0)`, `AUTO_TEMP (1)`, `AUTO_HUM (2)`, `MANUAL (3)`.
- **Directional Ventilation Gating:**
  - Cooling: Runs only if $\text{Outdoor Temp} < \text{Indoor Temp}$.
  - Heating: Runs only if $\text{Outdoor Temp} > \text{Indoor Temp}$.
- **Deadbands:** Temperature $0.5\text{ K}$, Humidity $3.0\%\text{ RH}$.
- **Floor Scaling:** Target $<5\% \implies 0\%$; Target $5\text{--}10\% \implies 10\%$; Target $\ge 10\% \implies \text{Target}$.
- **Response Ratios:** Quiet ($10\%/\text{K}$), Performance ($20\%/\text{K}$).
- **Degraded Fallback:** Primary sensor timeout triggers 5-minute hold timer, then falls back to `MANUAL` at last known speed with an alarm.

### 4. Deployable Health Check

* Switch device to `AUTO/TEMP` with setpoint $22^\circ\text{C}$ ($295.15\text{ K}$).
- Simulate indoor $25^\circ\text{C}$ and outdoor $28^\circ\text{C}$ (outdoor hotter than indoor). Verify fan target remains strictly $0\%$.
- Cool the outdoor probe below indoor. Verify fan starts, flap opens, and fan ramps proportionally to temperature error.

---

## Stage 8: Local UI (OLED Canvas & Rotary Encoder)

*Goal: Provide local visibility and control via display and encoder, treating them strictly as untrusted adapters.*

### 1. Architectural Scope & Deployment State

* UI layer contains **zero control policy or state variables** (`OBS-02`, `GLOB-41`).
- Rotary dial actions generate semantic commands submitted to the `CommandManager`.

### 2. Files Created

* `global/display/display_canvas.h` & `.cpp` (Reusable text/graph rendering template for SSD1306).
- `global/core/command_manager.h` & `.cpp` (Canonical command pipeline: validation, gating, commit).
- `nodes/eq/controllers/eq_ui_adapter.h` & `.cpp` (Translates encoder clicks/rotations into commands).

### 3. Concrete Parameters & Configuration

* **Hardware Inputs:** Rotary Encoder Pin A (14), Pin B (13), Button (23, pullup). SSD1306 on `bus_internal` (0x3C).
- **Sleep Timeout:** Display sleeps after 30 seconds of inactivity; waking on first turn/press without altering setpoints.
- **Encoder Dynamics:** 1 click = $\pm 0.5\text{ K}$ in Auto Temp; $\pm 1\%$ in Auto Hum; $\pm 5\%$ in Manual Fan.

### 4. Deployable Health Check

* Turn dial while unit is locked out by an interlock.
- Verify display shows setpoint command rejected/blocked, without bypassing the Operational Matrix. Display goes to sleep after 30 seconds and wakes smoothly.

---

## Stage 9: Network & Communication Adapters

*Goal: Implement Modbus RTU Slave and HTTP REST endpoints as thin protocol adapters.*

### 1. Architectural Scope & Deployment State

* Adapters deserialize external frames into canonical command structs (`GLOB-01`, `COM-01`, `COM-02`).
- Network drops do not affect ongoing autonomous climate regulation.

### 2. Files Created

* `global/comms/comms_contracts.h` & `.cpp` (On-demand queries `REP-013` and snapshots `REP-014`).
- `nodes/eq/comms/eq_modbus_adapter.h` & `.cpp` (Maps Modbus Holding/Input registers 0–99).
- `nodes/eq/comms/eq_http_adapter.h` & `.cpp` (Handles `/snapshot` and HTTP telemetry mailbox fallback).

### 3. Concrete Parameters & Configuration

* **Modbus Settings:** Slave ID `22`, UART TX 33, RX 32, Baud 9600, RS485 Flow Pin GPIO4.
- **Register Map Layout:**
  - Group A (0–7): Live telemetry (Int/Ext Temp & Hum, RPM, Fan %, Flap State, Health).
  - Group B (10–21): Controls (Speed target, Temp/Hum setpoints, Mode, Delta).
  - Group D (40–44): Identity & Diagnostics (Build version, bus errors, reset reason).
- **Failover Watchdogs:**
  - 60 seconds without poll: Flags `HubOffline` warning.
  - Triggers HTTP POST telemetry push with exponential backoff (10s, 30s, 300s).

### 4. Deployable Health Check

* Query Modbus registers with a USB-RS485 adapter from a PC.
- Verify register 0 reads internal temperature $\times 10$, and writing to register 11 updates target temperature atomically. Pull the RS-485 plug: verify EQ continues autonomous regulation uninterrupted.

---

## Stage 10: Inter-Node Compliance Purge Contract

*Goal: Implement the Thermostat ↔ EQ coordination protocol for mechanical HVAC purge assistance.*

### 1. Architectural Scope & Deployment State

* EQ enters `COMPLIANCE` mode upon a validated request from the Thermostat node (`CMP-01..04`).
- Automatically falls back to autonomous baseline if Hub or Thermostat communications drop.

### 2. Files Created

* `nodes/eq/controllers/eq_compliance_engine.h` & `.cpp` (Purge handshake, dynamic destination clamping, T3 timer).

### 3. Concrete Parameters & Configuration

* **Handshake Outcomes:** `ACCEPTED`, `NOT_USEFUL`, `RESOURCE_UNAVAILABLE`.
- **Equilibrium Band:** Terminate purge when $|\text{Indoor} - \text{Outdoor}| \le 1.0\text{ K}$.
- **T3 Timer:** Maximum run duration ($5\text{ minutes}$) starts only when fan physically begins moving air.
- **Hub-Loss Invalidation:** Missing heartbeat ping for $>60\text{ seconds}$ during an active purge immediately terminates compliance, brings fan to $0\%$, and reverts mode to `AUTO/TEMP`.

### 4. Deployable Health Check

* Send simulated `COMPLIANCE_REQUEST (COOL)` via HTTP while indoor is $26^\circ\text{C}$ and outdoor is $20^\circ\text{C}$.
- Verify handshake returns `ACCEPTED`, flap opens, fan ramps to high speed, and T3 timer counts down.
- Simulate network drop: confirm fan immediately halts and reverts to normal Auto mode after 60 seconds.

---

## Stage 11: Production Diagnostics, Live `/status` Web Endpoint & Final Lock

*Goal: Deliver full observability and complete the three-tier verification suite.*

### 1. Architectural Scope & Deployment State

* Provides a dedicated read-only `/status` diagnostic webpage (`OBS-01`).
- Freezes firmware build version and documents all register mappings.

### 2. Files Created

* `nodes/eq/controllers/eq_diagnostics.h` & `.cpp` (Aggregates subsystem health into structured JSON).
- Native ESPHome web server route registering `/status` (read-only GET, zero control buttons).

### 3. Deployable Health Check

* Navigate to `http://<eq-ip>/status` in a browser.
- Verify live telemetry stream showing:
  - Exact Kelvin internal values alongside display values.
  - Active Operational Matrix predicate states (E-Stop, Lockout, Flap interlock).
  - Measurement age of internal and external sensors.
  - Current authority domain for Fan and Flap.
- Run full physical test matrix (pull sensor wires, trigger e-stop, jam fan motor, power cut during NVS write) to ensure 100% graceful containment and recovery.

---

### Implementation Progression Summary

```text
[Stage 0: Barebones ESP] ──► [Stage 1: Types & Clock] ──► [Stage 2: Flash NVS]
                                                                   │
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
▼
[Stage 3: Resource Engine] ──► [Stage 4: Flap Servo] ──► [Stage 5: Fan Driver]
                                                                │
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
▼
[Stage 6: Fan↔Flap OM Safety] ──► [Stage 7: Auto Climate Brain]
                                          │
┌─────────────────────────────────────────┘
▼
[Stage 8: Local UI] ──► [Stage 9: Comms/Modbus] ──► [Stage 10: Compliance Purge]
                                                             │
                                                             ▼
                                                    [Stage 11: /status & Lock]
```

This sequence guarantees that:

1. Every stage produces a **compilable, flashable `.bin`**.
2. No higher-level control policy is written before its hardware drivers and safety gates exist.
3. Every component defines its specific defaults, variables, and Operational Matrix predicates at the exact stage of its implementation.
